# learner-model decision log

> Why learner-model is built the way it is. Each entry: what was decided, why, what was rejected, and in its tail "— *Consequence:*" the files, by their paths, that a change of it edits.
>
> **Rule:** do not propose again what was rejected without new facts. If a decision has to change, ask the author and rewrite its entry in its place, and every entry the change touches: an entry is its text in force — what was decided, why, what was rejected and what a change of it edits —, git keeps the old text, and the commit says why. An entry's number stands in its heading alone: an entry cites no other entry and no section, says in words what it rests on, and carries no date of who decided it and no amendment; a fact that no run of ours checks cites its one record of [sources.md](sources.md), and a measurement its record of [measurements.md](measurements.md). An entry that still has dated amendments is rewritten whole, its amendments folded in, when a task touches it.
>
> The repository was set up on 2026-10-19 from the template of `spec` v0.1.0; a move to a tag of `spec` is its commit, with no entry here. How it comes to differ from the template — the tools in the devcontainer, the rules in RUN.md — is recorded here as a decision. Next number — R49.
>
> Open questions that concern several repositories — О-, П-, Т- — are in the registers of `spec`, in `spec/docs/product.md`, `spec/docs/privacy.md` and `spec/docs/repositories.md`. What is still to decide in the component itself is in [architecture.md](architecture.md).

## The tag of `spec`

**R01. `learner-model` stands on `spec` v0.2.9: the submodule `spec/` is at this tag, the links to `spec` pinned to a tag name it, and what came from the template is as in the template v0.2.9. Since then the documents are checked by `just -f spec/justfile docs-check-repo`.**
- **What the tag brings to `learner-model`** (`git diff v0.1.0 v0.2.9` in `spec/`, read 2026-10-20):
  - the graph types are set by `learner-model`, and `engine` takes them from it; they are its own and simple, not from `module-kit`. Whether `engine` takes the types from here is decided; which types they are is decided here, checked against what `engine` needs;
  - the success corridor is a rule of `engine`: `learner-model` gives the probability of a correct answer, and which corridor to aim at, `engine` decides; `spec`'s documents of the repositories and its vocabulary were corrected to fit the card. Where the threshold "mastered" lives — in standalone it is judged by the middle of the corridor — and how it follows the target of the corridor, `spec` left open: `learner-model` and `engine` decide it together, and a change of one passes `sim` together with the other;
  - standalone in `spec`'s vocabulary: when looking for something new, the rule there skipped a mastered topic until all reachable ones were mastered, while a topic returned to the rotation earlier too — when its recommended point rose above the level at which the topic was mastered, or when two errors in a row removed "mastered" from it. The phrase that a mastered topic did not return to the rotation is gone. The vocabulary has the terms "repetition", "rank" and "interface accessibility", and its item "Accessibility" became "Opening a skill and access to content";
  - a repository's documents are checked by the `docs-check` of the `spec` its submodule stands on: links, its own sections and log, the tables of questions and of debt, and the citations of `spec` — by the submodule at its tag. An example of a citation is written in inline code so that the check does not take it for a citation;
  - the CI checks are the same for all repositories, and `learner-model` has them even before code: secret scanning, DCO, CodeQL, Scorecard and the documents check — the shared checks from `.github`, and with them SonarCloud. All but Scorecard hold the merge into `main`, only through a pull request and only by a merge commit. `spec`'s session writes the CI files here, together with `dependabot.yml` and the exclusion of `spec/**` for SonarCloud, and sets the ruleset of the branch `main`. The Go checks and with them the shared license check come with the first code;
  - an analyser's finding that a fix cannot close stays open, and its reason is recorded: the reasons shared by all repositories in `spec`'s log, a repository's own in this log. RUN.md has the findings of SonarCloud, CodeQL and Scorecard from the previous task closed before a new one;
  - the release of `spec` comes out by itself, after green CI on `main`: the next patch of the line, immutable, with a GitHub attestation. A repository still moves to a new tag in a task of its own;
  - the shared rules: an example of a citation is written in code; a development or CI tool that ships nowhere and puts none of its own code into what ships may carry another OSI-approved license too, or free terms for open source, but only as a named exception — in the license check or in the decision that brings it in. This concerns the tools of the code of `learner-model`;
  - the random generator and the derivation of seeds are one for the whole platform: `module-kit`, the widgets, `engine` and `sim`. `learner-model` is not in this list, but its seed too comes from outside: if the model needs randomness, the generator and the derivation of seeds are the same ones, not its own;
  - `spec`'s decisions on other components — the key for the eval, the page of samples, the account with the owner's profile and the children's profiles, the engine's reason codes — do not change the documents of `learner-model`, nor do the new questions Т-27, Т-28 and П-17 and what `spec` changed on the API contracts, on the tags of `spec`, which now only the release workflow sets, and on the organization's security settings;
  - devcontainer — the base image `devcontainers/base` 3.0.10 by digest; Claude Code is installed without package scripts, its `install.cjs` is run by name, and `claude --version` stops the build on a stub; `git.alwaysSignOff` — a commit from Source Control is signed off by DCO.
- **What is changed here:**
  - `spec/` — at v0.2.9, commit 5eafb1a. The links to `spec` in README.md and the tag in CLAUDE.md and RUN.md — v0.2.9. That the repository was set up from the template v0.1.0, the opening of the log still says: this is history;
  - the examples of citations of the old template are in inline code, in the five lines where `docs-check-repo` finds them: two in CLAUDE.md, two in RUN.md and one in the opening of the log. RUN.md and CLAUDE.md name `docs-check-repo`, and RUN.md the rule on the analysers' findings. These places are as in the template v0.2.9;
  - `.devcontainer/` — the one that the recipe of `spec` v0.2.9 (`editDevcontainer`) builds for `learner-model` of kind `go`. The name of the container and of the network, `postStartCommand` and the Go extension are its own, as on v0.1.0. The move introduces no differences from the template;
  - what the tag decided for `learner-model` is named in its documents: RUN.md — the tasks of the model and of the graph types, and the question on the corridor left table 3; [architecture.md](architecture.md) — the random generator, the graph types, the corridor and standalone's rotation.

— *Why now:* `spec`'s session connects the shared checks from `.github` here, and among them the required "Documents / docs-check-repo". The shared `documents.yml` builds the stage `toolchain` from the `.devcontainer/Dockerfile` of whoever called it and runs `just -f spec/justfile docs-check-repo` in it ([`documents.yml`](https://github.com/altnovatrail/.github/blob/ff2a359/.github/workflows/documents.yml), release v0.1.0 of `.github`, commit ff2a359, read 2026-10-20). And this recipe exists in `spec` only since v0.2.3: on v0.1.0 the required check would be red and would hold any merge. v0.2.9 is the latest release of `spec` as of 2026-10-20 and immutable (`gh release view -R altnovatrail/spec`); `gh release verify v0.2.9 -R altnovatrail/spec` confirmed it by its attestation, the tag is at commit 5eafb1a. `spec`'s plan asks a move to take the latest release at the time of the task.

— *Rejected:*
- staying on v0.1.0 until the first code: the documents check would hold any merge, the model and the graph types would be decided by a `spec` that does not yet say who owns the corridor and the graph types, and a link to a section of `spec` that does not exist at the tag, or a gap in the log, would pass unnoticed;
- v0.2.6, on which `module-kit` and `tutor` stand (the submodule `spec` on the `main` of each, the GitHub API, 2026-10-20), or another tag older than v0.2.9: the move takes the latest release, and the template has the rule on the analysers' findings only since v0.2.7, and `spec` its reasons shared by all repositories only since v0.2.8;
- moving without the devcontainer or carrying its changes over selectively: the container of `learner-model` is the container of `spec`, and a difference would have to be recorded as a decision, and there is no reason for one;
- examples of citations in guillemets, as before: `docs-check-repo` checks a number in guillemets against the log, and `spec` rejected not checking them.

— *Consequence:* `spec/`; `CLAUDE.md`; `README.md`; `RUN.md`; `.devcontainer/`; [architecture.md](architecture.md).

**R02. `learner-model` stands on `spec` v0.6.3 — the latest release of the line 0.6, the move to which `spec`'s cross-repository plan puts first in the turn of `learner-model`. The tag closes П-7 and Т-22: the learner model lives in the database of `api`, as an append-only evidence log, and the evidence and the recomputation are decided here under one condition — the state appended by new evidence matches the one recomputed from the log. The new item of the card — knowing an anchor skill — is decided here by a task of its own.**
- **What the tag brings to `learner-model`** (`git -C spec diff v0.2.9 v0.6.3`, read 2026-11-02):
  - the learner model: П-7 and Т-22 are closed. It lives in the database of `api`; the append-only evidence log is the source of truth, and the skills' state is a cache with a mark of the version of `learner-model` and the release of `modules`. Evidence is one per finished attempt: what is in it and how much it weighs, `learner-model` decides, and `api` stores it as it is. The condition for `learner-model` is one: "the state appended by new evidence matches the one recomputed from the log", and if `learner-model` decides otherwise, Т-22 returns. The engine choice also stores the release of `modules` and the number of the last piece of evidence. How `api` stores all this, `api` decided ([api@d896943](https://github.com/altnovatrail/api/blob/d896943/docs/decisions.md)). This is the input of the evidence and its log;
  - the card of `learner-model` in `spec` — five items "To decide when detailed" instead of three. "Evidence log" became the item "evidence: what is in it and how much it weighs" with the condition above, and the new ones are knowing an anchor skill and lessons. Lessons are an item of `engine`: of it, the learner model takes only that a worked example does not serve as evidence;
  - the skill graph. Assessability is `verdict`, `rubric` or `none`, and a skill `none` is only ever supporting; the tier is computed from the graph, and `module-kit` puts it into the part of the package for the server. The transfer after a major version of a module is by `retired_skills`: the evidence of a retired skill goes to each of its replacements, and the weight is decided by `learner-model`; `[]` — nowhere. The table is closed: the server carries progress over. `api` decided that the transfer is part of every computation from the log, and it gives the graphs in the types of `learner-model` ([api@d896943](https://github.com/altnovatrail/api/blob/d896943/docs/decisions.md)). This is the input of the evidence and its log;
  - anchor skills: Т-14 is closed. The shared taxonomy is a vocabulary in the module format with IDs `core.<name>`; in format 0.2 there are four anchor skills. The model knows an anchor skill by this ID, and whether the learner masters it, it decides by the skills that teach it in the learner's modules. An anchor skill is never a primary skill of a task, but it can be a supporting one. This is the input of knowing an anchor skill;
  - the task: primary and supporting skills, and an item's own skills; the difficulty band and the difficulty from 1 to 5 within the band; the starting point is the band of the learner's grade, and how far apart the bands are and where a learner outside the bands starts, `learner-model` decides; the guessing of a choice is 1/k by the number of options. The check kinds of v0 are `number`, `fraction`, `choice`, `predicate` and `rubric`, and an item's verdict is made up of the verdicts of its answer slots. A module's task has no provenance field: in a module everything is from the author. This is the input of the model and of the weights of evidence;
  - the module: the full ID — the module, the kind and the local ID, `novatrail/olympiad-math/skill/counting-gaps`, and by it the learner model knows a part of a module; the subject in the manifest is the code `mathematics`, `physics` or `writing`. The concept "subject area" is still not in the vocabulary. This is the input of the model;
  - the first slice: О-1 is closed. Olympiad mathematics 1–6, school mathematics of grade 3 on the workbook and short essays in English. The two maths are one subject area, and only the shared taxonomy links them; a series of items of one type is a page of the workbook; there are no language modules in the slice. This is the input of the model, forgetting and the weights of evidence;
  - the withdrawal of a version: the evidence of attempts on a version withdrawn because of a wrong key, `wrong_key`, no longer goes into the model, and it is recomputed from the log. This is the input of the evidence and its log;
  - a hint at the learner's request is the open question О-21: how it enters evidence is decided together with the weights of `learner-model`. Format v0 has no sketch "how to start". The model sees a task template as one task, whichever instance comes up, and О-12 is closed. This is the input of the weights of evidence and of the evidence and its log;
  - the random generator is PCG, and the places of randomness are as in standalone's harness; Т-18 is closed. Whether the model needs randomness, `spec` does not decide;
  - the cross-repository plan is `COORDINATION.md` at the root of `spec`. The move to a tag of the line 0.6 is the first task of the turn of `learner-model`; then the detailing, and then the code together with `engine` through the gate of `sim`, after the harness and its first tag. `learner-model` and `engine` take a new format version with a new `sim` by a move of their own, and hold the gate on the previous tag. The plan does not change the dependencies of the tasks in this RUN.md: the coordinator takes the numbers and the dependencies within the repository from here;
  - the template: the README badges, the rule of RUN.md on the language, and the container in CLAUDE.md — "Node.js, Go and just". The rules of RUN.md and AGENTS.md on a review in one round were taken here earlier, by commit b323261;
  - the shared rules: code comes with its tests, and with Codecov the merge waits for `codecov/patch`; an archive the Dockerfile downloads is checked against its sha256 sum, and an apt package is taken from a snapshot of the Ubuntu archive; a file of a NovaTrail release enters code or CI only after the recipe `release-verify`; the normative texts for authors are in English; the free terms of a tool are within two new bounds. Until there is code, of these only the rules of the Dockerfile apply, and the container of `spec` carries them;

  - the corridor and the graph types did not change: the threshold "mastered" is still decided here together with `engine`, and the graph types are its own, and `learner-model` sets them. The types `types/formatv0` of the Go module of `spec` serve `module-kit`, `sim` and `api`, and the rule to move the Go module of `spec` together with the submodule does not concern this repository as long as it does not import the module;
  - `spec`'s decisions on other components and tools — the interface and npm packages, samples, licenses, the review board, the schemas of the format, AI, reading and analysis, the canvas, the vocabulary term "solution", the solver, the TypeScript types, icons — do not change the documents of `learner-model`; of the module package, only the tier matters here, above.
- **What is changed here:**

## The learner model

**R03. The learner's level is per module, and standalone's correction for a topic belongs to the skill: the estimate of mastery of a skill is the module's level plus the skill's correction. The scale of the bands, the trial series, the step and the cautious "mastered" are as in standalone, the start is a band's width below the band of the grade, and guessing is by the item's answer slots. The threshold "mastered" lives in `learner-model`.** The whole model is in [architecture.md](architecture.md), its sources in [sources.md](sources.md).
- **What is decided:**
  - the level θ — each module of the learner has its own, on the scale of the bands of this module. The evidence of different modules does not add up in the level: only an anchor skill links the modules — by a prerequisite and by the field `teaches`. There is no subject area in the model;
  - the correction δ belongs to a skill of a module with the assessability `verdict`, and the estimate of mastery of the skill is θ + δ. The uncertainty of the estimate is v, as in standalone (sources 1.5);
  - the scale is standalone's numbers, one for all modules: the band at place p in the `bands` of the manifest stands at 2.5 · (p − 1), and a task of band p with difficulty d at β = 2.5 · (p − 1) + (d − 3). The bands overlap: the two hardest difficulties of a band reach into the two easiest of the next one (sources 1.5). The place of the band is taken in the version of the module on which the attempt went;
  - the start band of a module:
    - the one whose grades name the learner's grade;
    - a grade that no band names — the nearest band by grades, and at an equal distance the lower one;
    - without a grade and in a module without grades — the first band.

    The library's rule "band by grade" writes it into the evidence, and the append replaces it with the band from the state if the module already has one, and gives the evidence back with the band it took. The step — at the append and at the recomputation alike — takes the band from the state for a module with a state, and the band of the evidence for a module without one. So the module's first evidence pins it, and a change of grade does not move it afterwards, as in standalone (sources 1.5). Beside the place of the band — for the task's band and for the start band — the evidence carries the band's ID too, for a table of correspondence of bands if one is ever needed; the recomputation reads the place;
  - the starting point is θ₀ = 2.5 · (p − 1) − 2.5, a band's width below the start band: a learner of the lowest band's grades, or with no grade, starts at −2.5, a place below the lowest band;
  - an adult's correction of the module's level sets θ at the offset of the band set, and three failures of the module in a row lower θ by 2.5, not below −2.5;
  - the trial series is the whole pieces of evidence of the module while n < 5: θ is the point of maximum posterior probability, with a weighted likelihood, under a normal prior distribution with centre θ₀ and spread σ₀ = 2.5, found on a grid with step 0.05 and by bisection, and the δ do not move in the series (sources 1.5). Until the series is over, its answers lie in the module's state. It ends early when an adult corrects the level in it;
  - the probability of a correct answer of an item is P = c + (1 − c) · R̄ · σ(θ + δ̄ − β):
    - δ̄ is the mean δ of the item's primary skills, its own or the task's skills; supporting skills do not enter P;
    - R̄ is the retention of the item's primary skills at the moment of the answer, by the law of forgetting; while R̄ = 1 — in the trial series and for a skill without earlier practice — the module of olympiad mathematics computes exactly as standalone;
    - c is the product over the item's answer slots: 1/k for `choice` with k options, 0 for `number`, `fraction` and `predicate`. An item is correct only when all its answer slots are correct, as the module format says;
  - the answer of an item is its first verdict "correct" or "not yet" in the attempt, of an item all of whose answer slots are of a known kind: an item with an unfamiliar verdict or check kind is read without an answer;
  - the step is as in standalone, θ += K_θ · (S − P), δ += K_δ · (S − P), K_θ = max(0.2 / (1 + 0.05 · n), 0.05), K_δ = 0.4 / (1 + 0.05 · m) (sources 1.5), by evidence: one piece of evidence gives one step, from the state before it, summed over its N answers, each weighing u = 3 / (N + 2) and each S − P with the weight w of forgetting, w = 1 at R̄ = 1; the δ of a primary skill of the item gets 1/J of the step, for its J primary skills. β is not updated: the task's hardness is set by the author;
  - the model's counters — n and m in the step and in v, the five answers of the trial series and the five answers before "mastered" — are sums of the weights u of the answers of items with a verdict, with a tolerance of 1e-9, and m grows by the whole u. "The number of pieces of evidence" in the skill's state is the number of attempts;
  - n is no longer the number of answers in the module: after a pause of the module of 14 days or more it drops to 20 before the step, and with it the step of θ and the v of each skill of the module widen — the forgetting of the module's level. In the trial series n < 5, and the rule does not touch it;
  - "mastered" is as in standalone, with a threshold without guessing (sources 1.5):
    - it is set by a piece of evidence without a failure, with a correct answer without help, in a skill where m ≥ 5;
    - it is set at the highest of the bands where the skill is taught, not above the task's band, at which σ(λ − the band's offset) ≥ 0.71875 at the cautious level λ = θ + δ − √v;
    - it does not move down, and two failures in a row — pieces of evidence where the skill has fewer than half correct — remove it.

    0.71875 is P = 0.775 for a task with five options, the middle of standalone's corridor. The threshold is a constant of `learner-model`, and `engine` reads "mastered" and the mastery band;
  - the numbers of the step, σ₀ = 2.5, I = 0.149981 in v and the way to find the maximum in the trial series, by a grid and bisection, are standalone's numbers and solver (sources 1.5);
  - the operations are `BandByGrade(grade, bands)`, `Append(state, evidence, graphs)` and `Recompute(log, graphs)`. `Append` gives the state and the evidence with the start band it took, and `api` writes both in one transaction. The empty state is the zero `State{}`, because that is what `api` holds while there is no cache;
  - evidence without a single answer changes nothing — neither the module nor the start band: it moves nothing;
  - `Append` rejects, beyond the checks of the codec, everything the step relies on: without a check, a wrong value would silently land in a θ that no log would have given;
  - standalone's test vectors — the numbers of its tables at commit `6b29dd9`, read 2026-11-07, without code — hold for a task of one item solved without help and without a pause; the vector that goes from n = 0 by an Elo step, bypassing the trial series, a test inside the package checks.
- **Decided apart:** how the weight of evidence enters the step and the counters, what the supporting skills get, what a probe gives and which outcomes are "a correct answer without help" — the weights of evidence; the law of forgetting; the kind of evidence, the transfer and the append; the graph types; anchor skills and skills with `rubric` and `none`.

— *Why:*
- **The level per module.** Each module has its own scale of bands, and "numbers are the model's business, not the format's", as `spec` decided: the format does not compare the hardness of the tasks of different modules. The band "grade 3" of school mathematics and the band "3–4" of olympiad mathematics are not the same hardness, and a level per subject area would add up their answers on one scale. As the starting model, `spec` names exactly standalone's model with a level per module. The link between the two maths of the first slice is carried by the shared taxonomy, and the level does not need to carry it.
- **The correction belongs to the skill.** Standalone's topics turn into skills of the module of olympiad mathematics, and "mastered" and the state in `spec`'s vocabulary belong to the skill. This is a hierarchical model of adaptive practice: "we just correct the global skill by the concept skill" (sources 2.4).
- **Standalone's numbers.** The module of olympiad mathematics, where a task is a choice among five options, then computes exactly as standalone, and its test vectors are valid (sources 1.1). Its harness measured the rule "mastered": 3.7 % false against 63.5 % for the former rule, and 4.6 answers against 9.9 (sources 1.5). Standalone itself calls the step 2.5 and the spread of the series a guess and a number of the simulation — the harness checks exactly these.
- **The starting point in the log.** The grade lies in the profile, not in the log. A recomputation that read the grade would, after a change of grade, diverge from the appended state, and the appended state has to match the recomputed. Each piece of evidence carries the start band, not only the first: a withdrawal for `wrong_key` may cut off the first. The place of the band, not its ID alone, because the recomputation does not open the packages of earlier versions. The append takes the band from the state, so two first attempts of one module, started before and after a change of grade, do not set the appended and the recomputed apart.
- **The start a band below, the adult's correction and the guard** are the rules that `spec`'s card names in place of a calibration of the start, which waits for the data of real learners.
- **The first band without a grade.** A trial series with a spread of one band over five answers moves the learner by a band in either direction (sources 1.5), and a weak learner starting from the middle one would more often meet tasks beyond their strength.
- **Guessing by answer slots.** The guessing floor is Birnbaum's model: whoever does not know the answer "will guess, and, with probability c_g, will guess correctly" (sources 2.2). With a choice among k options, "for question with k options the probability of a correct answer becomes" 1/k + (1 − 1/k) · σ (sources 2.3). An item is correct only when all its answer slots are correct, so guessing it is guessing each one. For a free answer the learner sees no options, and `spec`'s vocabulary calls its guessing "about zero". Zero is exactly this "about zero", until the harness shows otherwise.
- **A threshold without guessing.** "Mastered" means "solves", not "guesses": a threshold on P with guessing would come earlier on a choice between two than on a number. And 0.71875 gives olympiad mathematics exactly standalone's rule.
- **The threshold in `learner-model`.** "Mastered" depends on the history of answers — five answers in the skill, an answer without help, two failures in a row — and `spec`'s vocabulary keeps it in the skill's state. A number of `engine` among the inputs of the computation would make the cache depend on what is not in its mark.
- **The answer of an item as the unit.** The probability is computed per item: an item has its own answer slots and skills. How much a page of many items weighs is the weights of evidence, and u, 1/J and a failure as fewer than half correct are theirs.
- **The zero state and the refusals.** `api` holds the zero state while there is no cache, so the fold starts from it with no step of its own; and a value the step relies on that no log could give is an error of whoever gave it, which the recomputation must not hide.

— *Rejected:*
- a level per subject area by the code `subject`: it needs a shared scale of hardness for the tasks of different modules and authors, and with it a term in `spec`'s vocabulary and a field of the next format version;
- a level per module, but the start of a new module from the level in a module of the same subject: the same translation of scales;
- the correction at the tier: the tier is computed from the graph and shifts together with it, and "mastered" belongs to the skill anyway. A correction at the tier and at the skill together: standalone's harness did not check such a model;
- an initial estimate by age, by self-assessment and by a short check — in the first slice:
  - age is stored only as a category, and the bands do not name it;
  - self-assessment belongs to language modules, which are not in the slice;
  - a short check is a choice of tasks, a rule of `engine`.

  All three are "for later" in [architecture.md](architecture.md);
- a step of the bands that the module sets — a field of the next format version; bands without overlap, with step 5;
- without a grade — the middle band;
- the start band only in the first piece of evidence, the band by its ID instead of its place, the starting point by the grade from the profile at each recomputation;
- the threshold as an input from `engine`; "mastered" that `engine` decides;
- the threshold σ ≥ 0.775 — stricter than standalone, and its harness does not carry over to such a threshold; the threshold P ≥ 0.775 with the guessing of the skill's tasks;
- a small constant of guessing for a free answer and a number of its own for `predicate`: there are no data for them;
- the minimum of δ and the product of probabilities over the primary skills: the first jumps from skill to skill, the second drops P with the number of skills at the same difficulty from the author;
- an attempt as one answer for the counters: it would decide the weights of evidence in advance;
- an I that depends on the guessing of each answer: the state would have to accumulate sums, and the harness did not check that. The price — on a choice between two, the uncertainty is understated ([architecture.md](architecture.md));
- the step built first by answers, on tasks of one item, and by evidence later: the golden vectors would then be rewritten;
- a constructor of the empty state and a requirement of kind 1 for the empty one: an extra step for everyone who calls, with no use for the fold;
- setting up a state for a module, with only the start band, by evidence without answers: the band would be pinned by evidence that the model did not read;
- silently passing the inputs that `Append` rejects: the recomputation would then hide an error of `api`.

— *Consequence:* [architecture.md](architecture.md); `CLAUDE.md`; `README.md`; `band.go`, `model.go`, `step.go`, `fold.go`, `rules.go` and their tests; `standalone_test.go`; `testdata/fold/`.

**R04. Forgetting is the retention of a skill whose stability grows with spaced practice: the probability of a correct answer of an item is multiplied by the retention, which falls by a power law with the time since the skill's last practice, and after a pause of a module the step of its level widens. The law and the constants are one for all modules. Forgetting does not remove "mastered" but gives a review date, and `engine` gets the estimate for the time of the choice and this date. The current time does not enter the cache.** The model is in [architecture.md](architecture.md), its sources in [sources.md](sources.md).
- **What is decided:**
  - a skill of a module with the assessability `verdict` has the stability τ in days and the time of the last practice t_last. The retention R(Δt, τ) falls with the time Δt since the last practice, and R(0) = 1. A skill without practice is not forgotten: its R = 1;
  - the module's level θ is forgotten through its weight: if from the greatest t_last of the module's skills to the time of practice of the piece of evidence no less than d₀ = 14 days have passed, then before the step n := min(n, N), N = 20 — the constants `levelPause` and `levelWeight`. So after a pause K_θ is 0.1, if n was greater than 20, and not the floor 0.05 at n > 60, and v is wider for each skill of the module. There is no new field — the module's time of last practice is derived from the t_last of its skills — and the state kind stays 1;
    - in the step the rule stands after the check "no answers", before the step of the series or Elo: evidence without answers and evidence that weighs nothing change nothing after a pause too. In the trial series n < 5 ≤ N, and the rule changes nothing. A module without skills has no pause;
    - evidence whose answers have no primary skills in the graph, J = 0, moves θ and narrows n but does not set t_last, so the pause of the next piece of evidence is still counted from the skills, and while only such evidence comes, n holds at 20;
    - at reading there is no rule: `Read` after a pause gives v by the former n until evidence comes;
  - the probability of a correct answer of an item is P = c + (1 − c) · R̄ · σ(θ + δ̄ − β), where R̄ is the mean R of the item's primary skills, as δ̄ is their mean δ. At R̄ = 1 this is the model's formula without forgetting;
  - the stability grows after a correct answer — the more, the lower R was — and falls after "not yet". Within a session R ≈ 1, and τ barely grows: practice in a row does not hold for long;
  - at a piece of evidence R is taken by the time of practice from the log — the time of the attempt's last action, which `api` stores, not the time of its closing —, and "for now" — at reading, by the time that comes from outside. τ and t_last are set by the log, and they go into the cache, while the current time does not;
  - the law and the constants are one for all modules: they are constants of the version of `learner-model`, and they change only through the harness;
  - forgetting does not remove "mastered": answers set and remove it. Time gives a review date — the moment when the cautious probability with retention, R · σ(λ − the offset of the mastery band), falls below the threshold 0.71875;
  - `engine`, at reading for the time of the choice, gets the P of an item with retention, for a skill — the estimate and the retention at that time, and for a mastered skill also "mastered" with the mastery band, the cautious probability with retention and the review date, with the mark "already" if the threshold does not hold even without forgetting. Whether it is "due", the engine decides by comparing the date with its own time;
  - the harness checks forgetting on the two maths of the first slice: the virtual learners have a calendar, and their forgetting goes by laws different from the model's law;
  - the stability is denoted τ, not S as in FSRS: S is the outcome of an answer;
  - a power law, as in FSRS-4.5: R = (1 + F · Δt / τ)^(−C), C = 0.5, F = 0.9^(−1/C) − 1 = 19/81. So τ is the pause after which R = 0.9;
  - τ₀ = 1 day — with the skill's first answer;
  - after a piece of evidence τ := max(τ₀, τ · (1 + g · (1 − R))^S̄ · (1/2)^(1 − S̄)), g = 15, where S̄ is the share of correct among the skill's answers in it: all correct, τ := τ · (1 + g · (1 − R)), and at R = 0.9 the stability grows 2.5 times; none correct, τ := max(τ₀, τ / 2);
  - a skill's R in a piece of evidence is one — from t_last to this piece of evidence — and τ changes no more than once per skill per piece of evidence. τ, the time of the last practice and the number of pieces of evidence change for the item's primary skills that have an answer in the piece of evidence — both when the solution is shown and for "left"; the evidence does not touch the supporting skills;
  - the step of θ and δ is w · (S − P), summed over the answers of the piece of evidence with their weights, w = R̄ · P₁ · (1 − P₁) / (P · (1 − P)), where P₁ is P at R̄ = 1: so the step without forgetting carries over into the model with retention, and at R̄ = 1 w = 1. The code computes w as R̄ · P₁ · (1 − σ) / (P · (1 − R̄σ)), with the factor 1 − c cancelled, and without guessing as (1 − σ) / (1 − R̄σ); at R̄ = 1, w = 1 and P = P₁ exactly, by a branch of its own;
  - in the trial series P is without retention, R̄ = 1 and w = 1, while τ and the skills' time of practice are written as usual, with R by the time of practice;
  - the date is no earlier than τ₀ after the last practice, and the model computes it at reading, from the cache and without the current time. It goes without a power: R*^(−1/C) at C = 1/2 is 1/R*². The second after rounding up is checked by the retention itself, and while R · q there holds the threshold, the date moves by a second;
  - Δt is from whole seconds: the difference is taken without overflow and no more than 2⁵³ s. τ is kept below the largest `float64`, and the date goes into seconds with saturation;
  - the retention at t₀, the cautious probability with retention and the review date are functions of the model, and `Read` calls them;
  - `Append` rejects a skill of the state whose τ is less than τ₀: a skill has a state only when it has had practice, and a τ below τ₀ would give R ≈ 0 and a step out of nothing.
- **Decided apart:** where the time of practice lies in the evidence, whether the recomputation and the append match bit for bit with the power in the formula of retention, and how τ goes by `retired_skills`; the form of the time, the date and "already" in the types; whether knowing an anchor skill takes retention into account. The repetition rule — when to give a skill with a date, how many repetitions a day, how they share the queue — is `engine`'s.

— *Why:*
- **Retention with stability.** This is how spaced repetition works: memory fades until practice, and practice grows its strength. In HLR "memory decays exponentially over time", and h is "the half-life or measure of strength in the learner's long-term memory" (sources 3.2). In FSRS "The smaller the value of $R$, the larger the $SInc$ value. This means that the spacing effect accumulates over time" (sources 3.1). A failed repetition then does not pass for nothing: τ falls by half, the skill's P is low, and it soon comes up for repetition again.
- **Mathematics is forgotten too, and intervals work in it.** By a meta-analysis of 53 articles, "there is substantial skill loss with nonpractice or nonuse", and "cognitive, artificial, and accuracy-based tasks" are lost more than "physical, natural, and speed-based" (sources 3.6). In an experiment on mathematics problems, practice in two sessions "had no effect on one-week test scores but virtually doubled four-week test scores", while nine problems per session instead of three "had virtually no effect" (sources 3.7). So τ grows from a correct answer after a pause, not from the number of answers in a row.
- **A power law.** "The course of forgetting was best described by a simple power function of time" among six laws (sources 3.5), and in FSRS the law is exponential — 0.9^(t/S) in its notation — in v1 and v3 and a power law since v4 (sources 3.1). This is how forgetting goes in Ebbinghaus too: "very rapid at the beginning of the process and very slow at the end" (sources 3.4).
- **The numbers.** C = 0.5 and F = 19/81 are FSRS-4.5's (sources 3.1). τ₀ = 1 day is the first interval of SM-2, "I(1):=1" (sources 3.3), and the first box of the Leitner system, "All cards start out in the 1-day box" (sources 3.2). g = 15 — so that at R = 0.9 the stability grows 2.5 times, as the initial E-Factor of SM-2: "With all items associate an E-Factor equal to 2.5" (sources 3.3). Halving is a step one box down in Leitner's system, where the intervals of the boxes double. There are no data for these numbers (Д6).
- **One for all modules.** There are no data by which a module's author would choose a speed of forgetting, and a field of the format must be known to the engine and the harness before the code. Constants by the subject's code put the subject's code into the core, against the criterion of the first slice. Language may need something else: forgetting is useful "particularly when we are modeling declarative knowledge (in contrast to procedural skills, which are the typical focus of student modeling)" (sources 2.3) — this is Д6.
- **"Mastered" is not removed by time.** Then it is a function of the log: the cache, knowing an anchor skill, the engine's prerequisites and the skill map do not change from the calendar alone, without a single answer. The evidence of forgetting is a wrong answer, and two failures in a row remove "mastered", as in standalone, and it does not move down (sources 1.5). Forgetting shows in P, in the cautious probability with retention and in the date.
- **The date and the estimate together.** `engine` asked what to take as the sign of repetition — a threshold of the estimate or a date. The model computes the date by the same threshold as "mastered", and the threshold stays one, in one library. And P with retention is needed by the corridor: after a long break the first task is within the learner's strength.
- **Time bypasses the cache.** `api` recorded that time is no transfer, that forgetting is decided here, and that if any of it enters the cache, the question returns. The cache is only what the log, the graphs and the version set. τ and t_last are set by the times of practice in the log, so the appended matches the recomputed. A repeat of the choice takes the time that the choice stores ([api@d896943](https://github.com/altnovatrail/api/blob/d896943/docs/decisions.md)).
- **The time of the last action.** `api` closes an abandoned attempt by a job after 7 days: by the time of closing, the clock of forgetting would start from the day of the job, not from the day of the work.
- **τ once per piece of evidence.** A series of items of one type "are not ten independent pieces of evidence", as `spec`'s vocabulary says, and practice in a row does not hold for long (sources 3.7). The ACT-R model comes to the same: "This mechanism limits long-term benefits from further practice at higher levels of activation and produces the spacing effect" (sources 3.8).
- **τ by the share of correct.** The step counts the skill's answers in a piece of evidence together, and at S̄ = 1 and 0 the rule gives exactly the rules for all correct and for none.
- **The weight of the step.** The step S − P is the derivative of the log-likelihood of the answer, brought to Elo. Retention multiplies this derivative by w, and w · (S − P) is the same step for the model with retention. A wrong answer after a pause then lowers θ and δ little — forgetting explains it — while a correct one moves them almost as without forgetting. Without w, a correct answer after 60 days at τ = 10 would shift θ and δ 3.6 times more than without forgetting, though the skill was not in doubt, and θ would inflate after a break for the whole module, as the example in [architecture.md](architecture.md) shows. The form of w in the code: the formula as it stands would divide small by small at small σ, and at R̄ = 1 the branch of its own keeps the bits of evidence without a pause.
- **Whole seconds and saturation.** The model gives the same bits for any state, whatever comes, and no NaN or ∞.
- **The date without a power.** Δt* goes through three roundings and otherwise could land a second before the threshold.
- **The date no earlier than τ₀.** A repetition on the same day barely grows τ: R ≈ 1. Without a lower bound, a skill mastered just barely, at q = 0.72 and τ = 1, would come up for repetition after 21 minutes, and so again and again in one session.
- **The trial series without retention.** Its answers lie in the module's state without time, and the skills it catches are almost all new: their R = 1 anyway.
- **g = 15 and halving τ stay.** The first judgement of `sim` rejected the candidates g = 10, the multiplier of τ 0.35 and both together: all three are better than the base by the estimate, but they fail the constraints "no worse", and the common failure is the catch-up after a break `M3.settle` on `F-break`.
- **The forgetting of the module's level.** The analysis showed that the delay of the catch-up after a break comes from the step with retention with a θ that is not forgotten, while for the base the step of θ at n > 60 stands at the floor 0.05. The exploration: N = 20 and d₀ = 14 hold all the constraints by the rule of the judgement of `sim`. The estimate is +0.0103 [0.0052, 0.0152], the group `jumps` +0.021, the catch-up `M3.settle` on `F-break` faster by 12.7 answers on olympiad and by 42.6 on school, and the corridor `E1.corridor` and `E2` are not shifted anywhere. The price: `M6.never` on olympiad is worse by 0.0025–0.0036 on five `F-` populations — v is wider, and "mastered" comes later — and `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school by 1.26 answers at a tolerance of the judgement of 2.61. The candidate `theta` went through the second judgement of `sim`, `model-2`, which chose it; the confirmation on the held-out learners `held-out-model-1` passed, and the bands of `sim` were recorded at the candidate's commit before the merge ([sim@7c32016](https://github.com/altnovatrail/sim/blob/7c32016/gate.json)). The objection that the time of the last practice belongs to the skill is removed by the module's time being derived from the skills: each piece of evidence that moves a skill sets its t_last. The rule differs from the growth of v with time, rejected below, in that it acts only with evidence: the module's v is wider, but it is still the answers that move the estimate.
- **The harness on mathematics.** There are no language modules in the first slice, and mathematics is forgotten too (sources 3.6). Standalone's harness knew no time: "every tenth answer starts a new day" (sources 1.3).

— *Rejected:*
- a correction for time only at reading, with the step without forgetting as it was: any answer would reset the clock, a failed repetition would barely move the estimate, and the intervals would not grow — a hundred mastered skills would give about fifteen repetitions a day without end. Pelánek's correction for time is not a law of forgetting but a "temporal increase in memory activation due to (short) time from previous exposure of an item", on facts of geography (sources 3.9);
- growth of the uncertainty v with time, as in Glicko, where RD grows "from the passage of time when not playing" (sources 3.10), and in Math Garden, where the uncertainty grows by 1/30 for each day since the previous answer (sources 2.3). The estimate θ + δ would not fade, and the corridor would not see forgetting. And the skill's part of v is no more than 1/(20 · I) ≈ 0.333, so the cautious level would fall at most by √0.333 ≈ 0.58 logits, and at n = 200 and m = 50 by 0.25. A skill mastered with a larger margin would never return for repetition;
- FSRS whole — 17 weights in FSRS-4.5, 21 in FSRS-6 (sources 3.1) — and HLR, where the weights of the features are learned from logs: the weights are fitted to cards and words, and there are no learners' data for them;
- the activation model of ACT-R (sources 3.8): it needs the whole history of the skill's practice with times and a fitted rate of decay;
- an exponential law, as in HLR and in FSRS v1 and v3: a power law describes forgetting better (sources 3.5);
- g = 10, the multiplier of τ 0.35 and both together: above, "g = 15 and halving τ stay". Proposing them again — only with new facts about the catch-up after a break;
- for the forgetting of the level, N 10 and d₀ 7: they break `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school; N 40 and d₀ 28 hold everything, but more weakly;
- the forgetting of the level together with the numbers of forgetting `forget-set` or with one base of halving τ, 0.35 or 0.4: no set holds all the constraints;
- a separate step of θ after a pause, by a new field of the state, that would not touch n and v: this is state kind 2 and a recomputation of the cache of `api`, and the exploration holds the price of n anyway;
- the forgetting of the level at reading, where `Read` would narrow n by the time of reading: this is the growth of v with time, rejected above, and the exploration did not measure it;
- a step without forgetting and w = 1;
- a speed of forgetting per module — by a field of the next format version — and by the subject's code: above;
- "mastered for now", at reading, and a mastery band that goes down as forgetting proceeds: "mastered" would change with the calendar, and the band would go down, though it does not move down;
- only the estimate for now for `engine`: the threshold "due" would live in the engine, a second one beside the threshold "mastered". Only the date: the corridor would not see forgetting;
- waiting for language modules, with forgetting 0 until them: there would be no repetition of the mastered even in mathematics, and the harness's criterion for it would be written later than the first candidate, while a criterion is written before the candidates;
- R̄ as the product or the minimum of the R of the primary skills: for the same reasons for which the model rejected them for δ̄;
- the step S − P with P with retention, without the weight w: a correct answer after a pause would inflate θ and δ — above, "The weight of the step". The step S − P with P without retention: a wrong answer after a pause would lower θ and δ as if there were no forgetting;
- computing w by its formula as it stands: above, "The weight of the step";
- a date without a lower bound: above, "The date no earlier than τ₀";
- τ by each item of the page: practice in a row would grow τ, against the experiment on mathematics problems (sources 3.7);
- the rules of τ only for "all correct" and "all not correct", and the mixed case by a temporary rule that would be rewritten later together with the vectors;
- all the reading in `Read`, with the retention, the cautious probability and the date computed there: the date would leave the rule of forgetting that sets it;
- a reset of τ to τ₀ after a wrong answer, as in SM-2, where then "start repetitions for the item from the beginning" (sources 3.3): one error is not yet forgetting, and only two failures in a row remove "mastered";
- silently raising a τ below τ₀ to τ₀: the recomputation would then hide the error of whoever gave the state.

— *Consequence:* [architecture.md](architecture.md); `CLAUDE.md`; `README.md`; `model.go`, `step.go`, `readout.go` and their tests; `testdata/fold/`; [run/DEBT.md](../run/DEBT.md).

## The tag of `spec` v0.6.6

**R05. `learner-model` stands on `spec` v0.6.6 — the latest release. The tag brings the showing of the solution, the hint on request, the outcome "left" and the facts of evidence that the weights of evidence read, and the conditions of the transfer that the evidence and its log wait for.**
- **What the tag brings to `learner-model`** (`git -C spec diff v0.6.3 v0.6.6`, read 2026-11-02):
  - the solution and the hint: О-7 and О-21 are closed. The solution is shown by a button: to a child — from the fourth wrong answer, to a learner of the category `adult` — from the start of the attempt, and with the adult's setting "ask before showing", on by default, — by the adult's action. The showing closes the attempt with the outcome "solution shown" — "not solved", without a trap of its own. A request before the first wrong answer gives "what is known and what to find" and the task's technique, after it — the words of the current rung once more, and "explain differently" — the same rung in other words. The outcome "left" belongs to an attempt that the learner left or abandoned when there were wrong answers in it. Beyond the outcome, the evidence carries the traps the program found, the items correct to the end, with their verdict, the technique — at a step or on request —, the number of "explain differently" and whether the solution of this task — not the template — had gone to the device before, and the weights of the outcome and of each fact belong to `learner-model`. `spec`'s vocabulary, the card and the module format say the same. This is the input of the weights of evidence;
  - the conditions of the transfer, `spec`'s answer to `api`'s question on them:
    - a skill of a module that is in neither `skills` nor `retired_skills` of the current version goes nowhere;
    - an anchor skill goes as it is if the vocabulary of some format version in the window of `api` knows it;
    - the skills of a trap — by the same rules;
    - in the cache is only what the log, the graphs and the version of `learner-model` set, and the starting point of skills without evidence and knowing an anchor skill the model computes at reading;
    - evidence carries everything the recomputation reads, and the recomputation does not open the packages of earlier versions;
    - a snapshot of the state is valid only under the same mark of the cache, and the transfer is not applied to it.

    `spec` also says that `api` cuts off the evidence of `wrong_key` before giving the log, and the card now says so: the question "whose work is it" is removed from [architecture.md](architecture.md). And an attempt that was left or abandoned without wrong answers gives no evidence. This is the input of the evidence and its log, of knowing an anchor skill and of the weights of evidence;
  - a withdrawn version counts as published when versions are compared, and the withdrawal does not free its skill IDs. This is the input of the evidence and its log;
  - the sixth item of the card: a task — not a template — whose solution has already gone to the learner's device the engine does not give again at once, and after how long it gives it, `engine` decides. This is an item of `engine`, and here only the weight of such an attempt;
  - the session of `spec` finds the rows «в реестр spec» itself — before each task `just inbox` reads the tables 3 of RUN.md of the other repositories on their `main`. It recognizes a row by the mark «В реестр spec» at the start of the question, and the rows of table 3 here have it just so. The shared rules and the template's RUN.md now say the same. On v0.6.6 `just inbox` found two rows of `learner-model` new, and their triage was still ahead, so the tag closes no rows of table 3;
  - API contracts in protobuf between `app` and `api`, and in the Dockerfile of `spec` — buf 1.75.0 with sha256 sums. `learner-model` is a library, and it has no API contracts;
  - `spec`'s other decisions of the tag — about other components: an own key of an AI provider, the deployment configuration of `app`, the task's key on the device, "step ready" without reading the work, a chain graph for a sequential module — do not change the documents of `learner-model`;
  - `COORDINATION.md` still names `learner-model` on `v0.2.9`, without the tasks of the move. What the repository took by a move, the coordinator sees by the submodule.
- **What is changed here:**
  - `spec/` — at v0.6.6, commit 5d8765e; the tag in CLAUDE.md, README.md and at the start of RUN.md;
  - the template: a three-way merge of the template v0.6.3 → v0.6.6 (`newRepo` from `spec/tools/new-repo/new-repo.mjs` with `draft`, in copies of `spec` at both tags in a temporary folder) with CLAUDE.md, README.md, RUN.md, AGENTS.md and this file. Taken: the rule of RUN.md on the mark «В реестр spec»; the conflicts came only from our own places — the paragraph of README.md about the component and the opening of the log;
  - `.devcontainer/` — the one that the recipe of `spec` v0.6.6 (`editDevcontainer`) builds for `learner-model` of kind `go`. Only the Dockerfile changed — buf 1.75.0; there are no differences from the template, except buf in CLAUDE.md, below. The image was rebuilt, and its checks passed: `go version` — go1.27.3, `buf --version` — 1.75.0, `meta.env` — `VERSION='3.0.10'`, `claude --version` — 2.1.280;
  - what the tag decided for `learner-model` is named in its documents: [architecture.md](architecture.md) — what is still to decide and the model; RUN.md — the tasks of the weights of evidence, of the evidence and its log and of knowing an anchor skill, and in table 4 the debt of the tag and of the image is closed; CLAUDE.md, Environment, — buf in the container, as in the CLAUDE.md of `spec`, and the same for the template went to `spec` as a row of table 3.

— *Why now:* the weights of evidence weigh the outcomes and facts that the tag names, and v0.6.3 does not have them: the plan asked a move to a tag with them, by a task of its own, before the weights. v0.6.6 is the latest release as of 2026-11-02 and immutable (`gh release view v0.6.6 -R altnovatrail/spec`: published 2026-11-02T15:09:41Z, `isImmutable` — `true`); `gh release verify v0.6.6 -R altnovatrail/spec` confirmed it by its attestation, the tag is at commit 5d8765e. The same tag carries the conditions of the transfer that the evidence and its log waited for.

— *Rejected:*
- the weights of evidence on v0.6.3, citing `spec`'s decision on the solution in inline code: the documents check would not check the citations of what `spec` decided on the solution and the transfer, and the weights would be decided by a text that is not in the submodule;
- v0.6.5, the first tag with the showing of the solution: the move takes the latest release, and v0.6.6 brought beyond it the API contracts, an own key, the configuration of `app` and a fix of the shared rules about the inbox, which concerns this repository too;
