## Format 0.4 in the model

**R28. Format 0.4 in the model's code: the value `CHECK_KIND_TIME = 5` of `CheckKind` with guessing 0 and the field `leading_question_on_request = 20` of the evidence — help, as a technique on request. With the field came the evidence kind 2: it always writes the field, and in kind 1 the field is "not recorded" and is read as "there was no question". The state kind stays 1, the edit does not change the model's numbers, and the comments of the `.proto` are written for the reader of the export. In one pull request through the gate of `sim` and in one release.**
- **What was found in planning** (the code of `main` beafeaa; `sim` and `engine` v0.1.6, read 2026-11-11):
  - the model would read `time` as an item without an answer in one place — `knownChecks` (`step.go`), where the boundary was `CheckKindPredicate`. It is called by the item's answer, the check of an answer of the trial series in the state and `Readout.Task`, so one edit gives `time` to all three. Guessing is already 0 for everything that is not `choice` (`guess`, `model.go`); the codec writes and reads any value of `CheckKind`; there is no `switch` on it anywhere;
  - an attempt where all items are `time` did not move the module and lost its traps: `move` exits before `countTraps`. Now the traps are counted — for example `twelve-hours-off` of the format's example;
  - the field 15 `technique_on_request` is always written and required in every kind, and in the logic only `helped` reads it; the new field follows the same pattern, but number 20 is written last, after the traps (19);
  - the edit does not break `sim` and `engine` v0.1.6: their literals of `Evidence`, `Task` and `Slot` are by field names, and the translation of a package by `sim` refuses `time` with its own map until `sim` takes it. `sim` compares the bytes of evidence only between two runs, so the gate's numbers do not move: the packages of `modules` v0.2.0 have neither `time` nor leading questions.
- **What is decided:**
  1. **the value `CHECK_KIND_TIME = 5`**, in Go `CheckKindTime`, is a free answer without guessing. It is read the same in every evidence kind: a new value is already an addition that the rules of the kinds allow, and nobody wrote kind 1 with `time` — the value was not in the schema;
  2. **the field `leading_question_on_request = 20`**, in Go `Evidence.LeadingQuestionOnRequest`: whether the button "Hint" gave the task's leading question. If it did, every answer of the attempt is with help, through `helped`, as with a technique on request. The name follows the neighbouring `technique_on_request`, the number is the next free one;
  3. **the evidence kind 2**, `EvidenceKind = 2`. The kind changes when a field is added, and in the former kind the field is "not recorded", not "no" ([architecture.md](architecture.md)): `api`'s log holds records of kind 1, where the question could not be recorded, and they must stay distinguishable from records where it was not given. The writer writes field 20 only in kind 2 and newer, and always, `false` too; the strict reader requires it there and rejects kind 1 with field 20; `EncodeEvidence` and `Append` reject kind 1 where the field is set. So the bytes of kind 1 from the log after reading and writing are the same bytes, and "not recorded" is read as "there was no question" — as the former version read kind 1: before format 0.4 there were no leading questions. The state kind is 1: the state got no new fields;
  4. **the comments of the `.proto` are for the reader of the export**: `api` puts the `common.proto`, `evidence.proto` and `state.proto` of the version that computed beside the JSON of the log and the state, in `api/proto/novatrail/learnermodel/v1/`. All comments were checked: the headers name the repository and the export, the fields of help say that an answer with them does not set "mastered", the field of the technique at the step that the model does not read it, the level θ the scale of the bands, the kind that kind 2 was added. That the archive's files are the ones `api` puts into the export is said by the README and by [architecture.md](architecture.md): the release's text is written by the shared `release.yml` of `.github`.
- **Checked** (2026-11-11): `just ci-test` — coverage 98.2 %; `just ci-lint`; `just ci-proto` — `buf breaking` against `v0.1.4` is green, the codec and the schema agree both ways; `just ci-release`. The golden logs `testdata/fold/` of the five former logs — bytes of kind 1, as `v0.1.4` wrote them — did not change by a byte and give the same states, and the new `format04` shows that a page after a leading question moves the skill just as without it, but only the page without the question masters it. `just gate exact` from a clone of `sim` `v0.1.8` in the container on this code is green, all 69 numbers to the bit, 98 seconds.

— *Why so:* format 0.4 is out, `sim` and `modules` wait for this release by the order of `spec`'s `COORDINATION.md`, and `api` writes the field only with it. Kind 2 keeps the distinction that the rules of the kinds hold, and writing the field only in kind 2 is what keeps the codec's promise: what the reader accepted, the writer gives back in the same bytes.

— *Rejected:*
- kind 1 and the field only when `true` — less code, but "the question was not given" and "`api` did not record it" could no longer be told apart in the log, and the rules of the kinds require keeping exactly this distinction;
- field 20 always, in kind 1 too — the bytes of kind 1 from the log after reading and writing would become different, and the codec's promise would break;
- `requested_leading_question`, by `spec`'s contract — there it is a string with the text of the question in the conversation between `app` and `api`, and here it is a fact of the attempt beside `technique_on_request`;
- `CHECK_KIND_TIME` only in kind 2 — a needless condition in the step, and there is nothing to distinguish: kind 1 with this value does not occur.

— *Consequence:* the merge changes the release's `paths` and releases `v0.1.5`. `api`'s cache is marked with the model's version, so `api`, taking it, will recompute the states, but the edit does not change the numbers of the former logs. `spec`, `sim` and `api` learn of the release by a row «в реестр spec»; the debt of format 0.4 is closed. — `proto/novatrail/learnermodel/v1/`, `types.go`, `evidence.go`, `codec.go`, `codec_read.go`, `codec_check.go`, `step.go` and the tests, `testdata/fold/`; README.md; [architecture.md](architecture.md); `run/QUESTIONS.md`, `run/DEBT.md`.

**R29. `Readout.Task` is faster with the same P to the bit and the same refusals: an ordinary call copies nothing but the slice of the answer. Tags and items that are already in order are read as they are, the module is a substring of the ID, `transfer` gives the graph's skill without a copy, the shares of the transfer are gathered only by the step, and the retentions of the skills at t₀ and the starting point of a module without a state are computed once by `Read`. The API is the same; the reference is `Task` of version `v0.1.5` in the tests.**
- **What was found in the planning and the measurement** (the code of `main` 355affc; `engine` `main` d0bb4d5; 2026-11-11):
  - in `spec`'s debt of the speed of the choice, 1.34–1.43 ms is the time of the whole choice, not of a call: a third of it goes to 772 calls — about 0.6 µs per call. That is not the computation but the work around it: the profile of `go test -bench` with `-cpuprofile` gave `Task` 79% of the benchmark's time, and in it a notable share is memory allocation and garbage collection, while `detmath` is about 3.5%. Allocations: the copy with sorting of the tags in `idList` and `chanceOf` — 23%, `[]Skill{s}` in `transfer` — 12%, insertions of retentions into `rs` — 11% and of shares into `shares`, which `Task` does not read, — 11%, the copy of the items — 11%, `strings.SplitN` in `moduleOf` — 11%; and the concatenation `"task "+t.ID` before the check;
  - the package `olympiad-math` 0.1.1 from `engine`'s data: three bands, 19 skills, all with a verdict, 772 tasks of one item and one primary skill each, 32 of them with one supporting skill; answer slots — 569 `number`, 196 `choice` of five, 7 `fraction`; 19 templates. The package's tags in a task are in the module's order, so the path with a copy remains for tags out of order.
- **What was decided:**
  1. **checks without copies.** `idList` sorts a copy only if the tags are out of order (`sortedView`, beside `sortedCopy` in `graph.go`); `Task` checks the tags of the task and the items with the function `checkTaskTags` — which is `checkTaskContent` without the items' places — and checks the places on the items in order, with the same message; it copies and sorts the items only if they are not in place. `checkTaskPoint` concatenates the name only on a refusal. `moduleOf` finds the three `/` with the function `strings.IndexByte` and gives the module as a substring of the ID, with the same refusals as `SplitN`. The order of the checks and their messages are the same;
  2. **transfer without copies.** `transfer` gives the graph's skill as a slice of the graph itself, `g.Skills[i:i+1:i+1]` — it is only read — and `chanceOf` takes the item's skills without a copy if they are in order, and gathers the shares of the transfer into a slice the step gives it, and at reading into none. σ, R̄ and P are still computed by one function for the step and for reading ([architecture.md](architecture.md));
  3. **retentions at t₀ are in `Read`.** For each module whose current graph `Read` checked, it gives once the R at t₀ of each skill of the graph with a verdict, by ID, with the step's function `retention` — the R of the skill's state, or 1 without one — and the starting point of a module without a state from its own reading. `Task` only reads them: the transfer gives only skills of the graph with a verdict, so each R is found and none is inserted, and the slice is cut by `slices.Clip`, so that an insertion, were there one, would copy it. After `Read` the `Readout` does not change, and `Task` can be called from many calls at once, as the library has no global mutable state for the many requests of `api`. A candidate of a module that `Read` did not read goes the old path;
  4. **the reference is `Task` of version `v0.1.5`.** `task_reference_internal_test.go` holds its text and the texts of its checks word for word, but for the names and two lines: which modules `Read` checked, the reference learns from a new field, and it takes P from `chanceOf` the way the step calls it — R anew, into an empty list, and with the shares of the transfer: this is the very computation `v0.1.5` did at reading. The reference has no copy of its own of `chanceOf` and `transfer` — SonarCloud took it for duplicated code, 4.5% against a threshold of 3% — so the reference holds `Task`'s checks, its R and the starting point, which `Read` now computes, to `v0.1.5`, and P to the step. `TestTaskIsReference` compares `Task` with it to the bit and a refusal to the word: on each prefix of the golden logs in three readings of `readGolden`, on the random logs of the property and on the benchmark's pool, with each task of the log and its variants — tags and items in another order, a repeated tag and place, a choice of one option, options without a mark, place 0, difficulty 6, an ID without a module, not UTF-8, a module with no entry in the graphs and a module that `Read` did not read. `TestModuleOfIsReference` compares `moduleOf` with the former `SplitN` on IDs of every form. The test goes red if the time of the retentions is moved by a second or a word in a refusal is changed. `TestTaskAllocatesOnce` holds one allocation per call on the benchmark's pool: so it shows if the transfer gives a skill whose R `Read` did not compute — then P is the same, but the slice is copied; `TestTaskConcurrently` calls `Task` of one `Readout` from four goroutines at once, under `-race` in `ci-test`;
  5. **the benchmark** is `BenchmarkReadoutTask` in `bench_test.go`: a synthetic module of the shape of `olympiad-math` 0.1.1 and four states, as in `engine`'s benchmark: a new learner, the trial series, the middle of the way — ten skills — and a learner far along — 19 skills, 15 mastered, the review date of one has come. The whole pool per iteration; `ns/task` is a call.
- **Checked** (2026-11-11, AMD Ryzen AI 9 365, 20 threads, 10 runs each, the mean):
  - `Readout.Task` — 697–755 ns per call before the change and 286–346 after, 2.0–2.6 times faster; allocations — 10–12 per call before and one after, the slice of the answer; memory — 465–561 bytes per call before and 24 after;
  - `engine.Choose` on `main` d0bb4d5 — its `BenchmarkChoose` on `olympiad-math` 0.1.1 with a `replace` to this code: 0.96–1.04 ms per choice before and 0.57–0.69 after, 1.5–1.8 times; allocations — 9,538–11,046 before and 1,750–2,558 after, memory 54–63% less. `engine`'s tests on this code are green, the golden choices the same;
  - `just ci-test` — coverage 99.1%; `just ci-lint`; `just ci-proto`; the golden files `testdata/fold/`, `testdata/read/` and `testdata/wire/` — without changes.
  - the job "Gate" of pull request #56 on `sim` `v0.1.8` — all 69 numbers within their bands and to the bit the same as recorded; the session could not run `just gate exact` in the container. The release is `v0.1.6`, commit 7d796e2, checked by `gh release verify` and `release-verify`.

— *Why so:* `sim` will give the gate back 300 learners, and the deciding run 4,000, when the choice becomes faster, and raising the library's version costs it nothing. A change inside touches neither the API nor `engine`: `sim` and `engine` take `v0.1.6` by raising the version, and neither has anything to wait for.

— *Rejected:*
- a new API for many tasks — `Readout.Tasks`, or a task checked once when the pool is loaded: the gain could be larger, but `engine` would need a task of its own and a release of its own, and `sim` would wait for both;
- a lazy cache of retentions in `Readout`, filled by the first `Task`: `Readout` would change after `Read`, and two calls at once would write to one memory, while the library keeps no mutable state that many requests share;
- dropping `Task`'s checks for the modules `Read` read: `Task`'s refusals are part of its contract, and without copies the checks cost little;
- changing `sortedCopy` so that it gives the slice itself: `NewGraph` puts its result into the graph, and the graph would share memory with the caller's input.

— *Consequence:* the merge changes the release's `paths` and releases `v0.1.6`. `api`'s cache is marked with the model's version, so `api`, having taken it, will recompute the states with the same numbers. `engine` and `sim` learn of the release by a row «в реестр spec»; the debt of a faster `Readout.Task` is closed. — `readout.go`, `step.go`, `fold.go`, `codec_check.go`, `graph.go`, `bench_test.go`, `task_reference_internal_test.go` and the other tests; README.md; [architecture.md](architecture.md); `run/DEBT.md`.

**R30. Epic E5 is the second round of the model's candidates. First the gate on a tag of `sim` with the sizes after `v0.1.6` and, in parallel, the analysis of why the model with the forgetting numbers of the first round takes longer to catch up with the truth after a break, `M3.settle` of `F-break`; then the exploration at the size of the deciding run, the drafts of the candidates, the judgement `model-2` and the summary. The round does not wait for the pin of `modules` v0.2.1. Each task is a branch of its own from `main`, a pull request and a merge of its own, which the executor takes to green CI himself; the drafts of the candidates wait for the judgement, and only the chosen one is merged. The epic is done, and its result is reached in full: "Gate" stands on `sim` `v0.1.10`, a tag with the sizes after `v0.1.6`; the cause of the slow catching up after a break is recorded; the judgement `model-2` chose `theta`, the confirmation on `held-out-model-1` passed, and `theta` came out as the release `v0.1.8`, commit ab688b2.**
- **What was found** (clones of `sim`, `engine` and `spec` on `main`, 2026-11-11):
  - `sim` judges second rounds by its epic «когда придут кандидаты» — the judgement `model-2`, the confirmation on the untouched `held-out-model-1` — and sees a candidate's second library. The round waits for no separate epic of `sim`;
  - E5 of `engine`, the second round of the corridor, goes, by the plan of `engine`'s epics, «после того, как `learner-model` сдвинет `M3.lag` или ускорит `Readout.Task`» — the second condition was met by `v0.1.6` —, and `engine` puts it «после `learner-model`». A candidate of the model shifts the engine's base, so which second round comes first is a question «в реестр spec»;
  - `sim`'s sizes — the gate 150 learners, the deciding run 1,500 — will be raised by its measurement after `v0.1.6`, and there is no task for it in `sim`'s plan yet;
  - the pin of `modules` v0.2.1 waits for a release of `modules`, and that one for `module-kit` on format 0.4, which has no epic;
  - in a Claude Code session the classifier did not let `sim` be run from a clone, and the exploration does not go without it.
- **What was decided:**
  1. six tasks: the gate on a new tag of `sim`; the analysis of `M3.settle` of `F-break` — without dependencies, in parallel with the gate; the exploration — after both, at the size of the deciding run; the candidates as drafts, the judgement and the release, the summary — as in E3;
  2. forgetting of θ (Д6) is in the exploration if the analysis shows that the delay in catching up comes from θ not forgetting over a break. It is a rule, not a number: a new field of the state would be a new state kind and a recomputation of `api`'s cache, which the author decides. It needed no new field: the rule goes through n, and the state kind is still 1;
  3. the pair with `engine` — the threshold "mastered", the target of the corridor and the bound ½ (Д7, Д16) — is not here but in epic E6. E6 opens when the threshold has a reason of its own, `engine` proposes its target and `sim` can judge the pair; none holds ([engine@3afe4b1](https://github.com/altnovatrail/engine/blob/3afe4b1/run/EPICS.md), [sim@c80e110](https://github.com/altnovatrail/sim/blob/c80e110/docs/decisions.md)). `engine`'s E5 ended without a judgement, and its plan of epics is empty until a reason comes; `sim` lays out a judgement only for a candidate with a reason of its own and stops long runs of fitting constants while there are fewer than 100 children; whether that stop covers the pair's judgement is the author's, asked when the other two hold. Work of `engine` or `sim` is not needed now, so there is no row «В реестр spec»;
  4. the round is judged on one pin of `sim`: the sizes by the measurement and the translation of `time` and the leading question before the gate, and the pin of `modules` v0.2.1 after the judgement and the confirmation. `sim` took `modules` v0.2.5 — with `time` answer slots and leading questions — by its tag `v0.1.9`, before the tag of the sizes, so the round was explored and judged on them, and the candidate for the weight of an answer with help was explored with leading questions. The text «Почему такой порядок» of E5 in its archive [plan/E5.md](plan/E5.md) speaks of the packages v0.2.0, and this entry is in force over it;
  5. each task is a branch of its own from `main`, a pull request and a merge of its own, which the executor takes to green CI himself, as in E4. The drafts of the candidates are branches from `main` with draft pull requests into `main`; the judgement's task merges the chosen one, and this merge releases a patch of the line `0.1`.
- **Checked** (2026-11-11): `just -f spec/justfile docs-check-repo` — ok; the archive [plan/E4.md](plan/E4.md) is the block E4 of RUN.md of revision 9f57425 word for word, but for the links. `/code-review high` over the whole branch of the summary of E4 and the unfolding of E5, one round — 10 findings, all fixed.

— *Why so:* the first judgement showed where the failure is but not why, and an exploration without the analysis would again choose the forgetting numbers that delay catching up. The analysis does not wait for the sizes, but the exploration does: the coarse look of the first round at 300 learners did not see the judgement's failures. A branch for each task keeps one release and one recomputation of `api`'s cache: the code of the round is only the drafts of the candidates, and of them only the chosen one goes into `main`; the other tasks change CI and documents, outside the release's `paths`.

— *Rejected:*
- the exploration at once, at the present sizes: its numbers would change with the tag of `sim` after the measurement, and the exploration would have to be repeated;
- waiting for the pin of `modules` v0.2.1: its date depends on `module-kit`, which has no epic, and the numbers of Д5 and Д6 do not depend on it;
- the pair with `engine` in this same round: `engine` has not proposed its half — the target of the corridor — and its E5 is the variants of the corridor at the same threshold;
- one branch `e5` for the whole epic, each task a commit in it, with one merge into `main` after the summary: it gives no fewer releases than a branch for each task, since the code of the round reaches `main` only with the chosen candidate.

— *Consequence:* E4 and E5 are in the archives [plan/E4.md](plan/E4.md) and [plan/E5.md](plan/E5.md). — RUN.md, `run/EPICS.md`, `run/QUESTIONS.md`, `run/DEBT.md`; CLAUDE.md; README.md; [architecture.md](architecture.md).

## Catching up after a break

**R31. With the forgetting numbers of the first round the model takes longer to catch up with the truth after a break because it believes in forgetting more strongly: an answer after a pause with a low R̄ says less about the level, and the module's θ is not forgotten over a break, so a fall of the level, which the law of retention does not describe, it puts down to forgetting for longer. This is a property of the step with retention, not a divergence of the model's law from the law of the harness's truth. Forgetting cannot be removed from the step: on populations without a fall the model then takes forgetting for a fall of the level. Catching up is shortened by forgetting of θ — a wider step of θ after a pause of the module of 14 days — and it needs no new field of the state. It goes into the round's exploration on its own and together with the forgetting numbers, and the numbers that speed forgetting up are not explored without it.** The analysis of epic E5, which the round's exploration waited for.
- **The harness** is `sim` `v0.1.8`, commit d957fc7: `engine` `v0.1.6`, `modules` v0.2.0, the set `working`, `engine`'s rule, 1,500 learners per graph of each population — that is the size of the deciding run at this tag ([sim@d957fc7](https://github.com/altnovatrail/sim/blob/d957fc7/justfile)) — on both maths. The run is assembled as the recipe `decisive`: a `go.work` in the session's folder with a clone of `sim`, a variant of `learner-model`, and `engine` at the commit of `gate.json`, 20 parts `sim run -populations … -learners 1500 -part k/20 -commit …` and `sim merge`; the comparison is `sim compare` against the base, paired by learner, with a 95-percent interval. The base is `learner-model` `v0.1.4` from `sim`'s `go.mod`: `v0.1.6`, which only made `Readout.Task` faster, gives the same bits. The candidates of the first judgement are the commits of the drafts #43–45: `g10` 7f735b2, `halve035` 17e0a42, `forget-set` 75c6a90. The diagnostic variants are commits of the clone in the session's folder; neither they nor the files of the runs go into the repository, and they were never published anywhere, so a repetition assembles them anew by the description below — then the numbers are the same, and the file's header names its own SHA:
  - `w1`, 7847d28 — `forget-set` and w = 1 for each answer in the step — the field `w` in `answers`, `step.go` — and P with R̄;
  - `nostep`, 4b95d26 — `forget-set` and a step without forgetting: in `answers` P₁ instead of P and w = 1, while τ and reading are as they are; `nostep-base`, 2b69d24 — the same on top of the base;
  - `theta`, 235e44d — the base and forgetting of θ: in `move`, right after `at := e.PracticeTime`, outside the trial series and for a module with skills, if from the greatest t_last of the module's skills to the time of practice is no less than 14 days, n := min(n; 20) — K_θ 0.1 instead of the floor 0.05, and a wider v; `theta-fs`, 3c603ab — the same on top of `forget-set`;
  - `law` — the truth of `F-break` in the clone of `sim`, commit 8c0285c on top of `v0.1.8`, with the law of `forget-set`: `a.Growth = 10` for `F-break` in `internal/population/population.go` and the base 0.35 instead of 0.5 in `internal/learner/attempt.go`.

  The SHA-256 of the files: the base on `F-break` — `12441811f9e5913c…7f5`, and a repetition gave the same file to the byte; the base on `F-model`, `F-none`, `F-exp`, `F-C1` and `F-growth5` — `9a9618339e07ed35…b79`; `g10` — `7bed24d5d11defa9…8f6`, `halve035` — `9db8026222be4046…ef1`, `forget-set` — `1cc325663d9961e4…595`, `w1` — `197998a6d6f68740…a38`, `nostep` — `015a44bf99827aa0…702` and on the five others `8e708bd37d2ea344…54c`, `nostep-base` — `fa5fe8efc0c7d935…f66` and `9e462fa825b97240…997`, `theta` — `a52a27a3c893f1af…320` and `e38b4b538902d076…4fc`, `theta-fs` — `340d89a87bb7a818…d51` and `70161c8e9570f5ba…aba`; on the truth `law` — the base `5a6563097aa625ae…fcf`, `forget-set` `42a776f9ae9c2877…2f4`. A run of `F-break` took from 3 to 10 minutes on 20 cores, of the five other populations from 16 to 38.
- **The yardstick.** "Not worse" is read by the rule of `sim`'s judgement (`notWorseChecks`, `internal/criterion/judge.go` of `sim`): the worse edge of the 95-percent interval of the paired difference is no farther than the tolerance, and the tolerance is the larger of the author's tolerance (`defs`, `measures.go` of `sim`: for `M3.settle` 1 answer, for `M1.rms.200` 0.01, for `M5.caught` 1) and 4.3 standard errors of the difference. The standard error here is taken from the interval of `sim compare`, its width divided by 3.92, and the checks are those the model's criterion puts on the `F-` populations. This is the judgement's yardstick on the `F-` populations alone, not the judgement: the analysis did not run the other populations.
- **What the runs showed** — `F-break`, b − a against the base; `M3.settle` — answers until catching up, `M3.unsettled` — the share of those who never caught up, `M3.lag` — the estimate minus the truth from the 101st answer; the last column is the "not worse" checks that the variant fails by the yardstick above, on `F-break` and, where there was a run, on the five other `F-`:

  | Variant | `M3.settle` olympiad | `M3.unsettled` olympiad | `M3.lag` olympiad | `M3.settle` school | "Not worse" failures |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | base | 173.1 | 0.237 | 0.280 | 338.1 | — |
  | `g10` | +13.5 [7.4; 20.1] | +0.011 [−0.011; 0.032] | +0.021 | 0.0 [−1.8; 2.0] | 1: `M3.settle`, 20.09 at a tolerance of 13.91 |
  | `halve035` | +6.3 [−0.7; 13.0] | +0.022 [0.001; 0.043] | +0.014 | +1.8 [−0.6; 4.4] | 0 |
  | `forget-set` | +22.2 [14.9; 29.5] | +0.009 [−0.015; 0.031] | +0.034 | +1.5 [−0.2; 3.3] | 3: `M3.settle`, 29.51 at 15.97; `M1.rms.200` of olympiad; `M5.caught` of school — on the edge |
  | `w1` | +28.0 [20.9; 35.8] | +0.065 [0.040; 0.088] | +0.060 | −3.7 [−7.0; −0.4] | 5 |
  | `nostep` | −51.0 [−58.2; −44.0] | −0.044 [−0.067; −0.022] | −0.262 | −78.5 [−82.4; −74.8] | 3, and 20 on the five others |
  | `nostep-base` | −55.9 [−63.1; −48.7] | −0.040 [−0.064; −0.017] | −0.266 | −78.0 [−81.9; −74.1] | 2: `M6.never` and `E1.corridor` of olympiad, and 19 on the five others |
  | `theta` | −9.8 [−15.0; −4.6] | −0.028 [−0.045; −0.011] | −0.020 | −42.2 [−44.5; −39.8] | 0, and 1 on the five others: `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school, 2.77 at 2.73 |
  | `theta-fs` | +10.0 [3.4; 16.8] | +0.005 [−0.019; 0.028] | +0.016 | −40.4 [−43.2; −37.6] | 1: `M3.settle`, 16.8 at 14.71; and 2 on the five others |

  On the truth `law`, which forgets by the law of `forget-set`, the base catches up in 162.1 answers, and `forget-set` 14.9 [8.2; 21.6] longer; on school maths 3.7 [1.1; 6.4] longer. The first judgement failed `g10` and `halve035` on `F-break` at 1,000 learners, on `engine` `v0.1.4`; here `halve035` holds the check: the harness is different — `engine` `v0.1.6` and the packages v0.2.0 — and that is for the judgement to decide, not the analysis.
- **Why so.** The step of θ is K_θ · w · (S − P), where P = c + (1 − c) · R̄ · σ, as [architecture.md](architecture.md) describes forgetting and repetition. The lower R̄, the less P depends on θ — the factor R̄ at σ — and the smaller w, so an answer after a pause moves θ toward the truth the more weakly, the more strongly the model believes in forgetting. After the break of `F-break` the truth's level falls by 1.0, while the module's θ is not forgotten: the model sees the fall only as wrong answers and, while R̄ is low, explains them by forgetting. The numbers of the first round grow τ more slowly and cut it harder, their R̄ after pauses is lower — and catching up is slower. Their calibration of P after pauses of 30 days and more is better at that: the bias `M2.bias.pause30` of the base is +0.048, of `forget-set` −0.011 — this is the same shift: the fall of the level is explained by forgetting, not by the level. What does not explain it:
  1. the weight w — `w1` catches up even longer: w damps the correct answer after a pause too, which with a low P would pull θ up;
  2. a divergence of the model's law from the law of the truth: the truth of `F-break` forgets by the law of the base — g = 15 and halving (`internal/learner/attempt.go` of `sim`) — and this would give the candidate an extra delay, but on the truth `law`, with the law of `forget-set` itself, the delay is 14.9 answers of 22.2: most of it comes from the numbers themselves. So there is no row «в реестр spec» for `sim`: `F-break` judges what it should.

  And the long catching up of the base itself — 173 answers, and a quarter of the learners of olympiad maths do not catch up — comes from θ not being forgotten and from the step of θ at n > 60 standing at the floor 0.05: `theta`, with a wider step after a pause, shortens it. So the condition the epic set for forgetting of θ is met: the delay comes from θ not forgetting over a break, which the step with retention after a pause hardly moves — and forgetting of θ goes into the exploration.
- **A step without forgetting is rejected.** `nostep-base` catches up on `F-break` 56 answers faster, but on populations without a fall of the level it errs the other way — it takes forgetting for a fall of the level, and θ goes below the truth: on olympiad maths `M3.lag` on `F-model` is −0.27 instead of −0.02, and `M1.rms.200` is worse by 0.14 on `F-model`, 0.19 on `F-exp`, 0.29 on `F-C1` and 0.23 on `F-growth5`, and by 0.02 on `F-C1` of school maths. On `F-none` of olympiad maths and on `F-model` and `F-none` of school maths its error is, on the contrary, smaller: there the truth does not forget, or forgets more slowly than the model believes. By the judgement's yardstick — 19 failures on the five other `F-` and 2 on `F-break`.
- **Forgetting of θ.** `theta` costs little on the five other `F-`, but not nothing: `M6.never` on olympiad maths is worse by 0.002–0.003 where it is outside the noise — on `F-model`, `F-none` and `F-exp` — v is wider, and "mastered" comes later; Brier and `M10.retention` are worse by less than a tenth of the tolerance; and one check fails by a hair — `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths, +1.5 [0.3; 2.8] answers at the judgement's tolerance of 2.73. Better are the false "mastered" on `F-none` and `F-C1` of olympiad maths and `M5.caught` on `F-exp` and `F-C1` of school maths. With `forget-set` the rule gives catching up back 12 answers of 22, but `theta-fs` still fails the check `M3.settle`. On the five other `F-`, `theta-fs` keeps the gain of `forget-set` in forgetting — `E3.late` is better by 0.03–0.12 on olympiad maths and by 0.29–0.36 on school maths, except `F-growth5`, where it is within the noise, and `M10.retention` on olympiad maths by 0.001–0.005 — but fails `M1.rms.200` on `F-none` of olympiad maths, 0.0204 at 0.014 — `forget-set` had the same failure in the first judgement — and `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths, 3.65 at 3.41. Worse than the base outside the noise it also has `M10.retention` on school maths — on `F-none` by 0.0014, two thirds of the author's tolerance, on `F-model` and `F-exp` by 0.0004–0.0005 — and `M1.rms.200` on `F-model`, `F-none` and `F-growth5` of school maths, by 0.0001–0.0006.
- **No field is needed.** The time of the module's last practice is the greatest t_last of its skills (`SkillState.LastPractice`, `state.go`): each piece of evidence that moves a skill sets its t_last. The rule, which acts on the first piece of evidence after a pause, reads it, and it lasts through n, which is already in the state. The price is the meaning of n: the "number of answers" becomes smaller after a pause, and with it, for each skill of the module, not only the one that came back, v is wider and the cautious level λ lower. `engine` reads this too — the estimate, the uncertainty and "mastered" through `Readout` — so the exploration measures the corridor `E1.corridor` and the measures `E2` too, not "mastered" and the review date alone: on `F-growth5` of olympiad maths `theta` has already shifted the corridor by 0.001, within the tolerance. A candidate with the rule changes the state and forgetting in [architecture.md](architecture.md), and the state kind stays 1. An edge: a piece of evidence whose answers have no primary skills in the graph, J = 0, moves θ but not t_last — then the module's pause is counted from the last practice of the skills, not from it; the candidate checks this with a test. A new field — a separate step of θ after a pause that does not touch n and v — is needed only if the exploration shows that the price of n does not hold "not worse"; that is state kind 2 and a recomputation of `api`'s cache, and the author decides it when the candidate is planned.
- **What was decided:**
  1. the cause of the delay is the step with retention with a θ that is not forgotten, not the numbers on their own and not the law of the harness's truth: numbers that speed forgetting up always delay catching up, and the exploration does not explore them without forgetting of θ;
  2. forgetting of θ goes into the exploration — n := min(n; N) after a pause of the module of no less than d₀ days — with two numbers: N, the strength, and d₀, the threshold of the pause. The diagnostics took N = 20 and d₀ = 14 — the threshold of a skill's pause in `sim`'s measures; the exploration looks for N and d₀ for the rule on its own and together with g and the base of the division of τ — in a set with `forget-set` the rule must give catching up back all 22 answers, not 12, and leave no failure of `M1.rms.200` on `F-none` of olympiad maths — and measures it on all populations, with `E1.corridor` and `E2`, and `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths;
  3. rejected: a step without forgetting — it breaks the error of the estimate on four of the five `F-` without a fall on olympiad maths and on `F-C1` of school maths; w = 1 — it catches up worse; a row «в реестр spec» for `sim` about the law of the truth of `F-break` — the delay comes from the numbers themselves, not from a divergence of the law.

— *Why so:* the judgement found the failure but not its cause, and an exploration without the cause would again choose the forgetting numbers that delay catching up. The runs went only on the `F-` populations — the break and the measures of forgetting are there — so the analysis took hours, not a day. The other populations know pauses too — a session a day with probability 5/7, and a skill waits its turn for days — and the analysis did not measure the price of the rules on them: the exploration measures it on all populations.

— *Rejected:*
- small runs on 300 learners, as in the exploration of the first round: 4.3 standard errors of the difference of `M3.settle` at 1,500 learners are 14–16 answers, and at 300 twice as many, wider than the difference itself;
- tracing the step through the answers after a pause: the runs told the hypotheses apart without it;
- a field "time of the module's last practice": it is derived from the skills.

— *Consequence:* nothing changes in the code, the tests and the documents of how it is built. — RUN.md; `run/DEBT.md`, Д6; [architecture.md](architecture.md).

## The exploration of E5

**R32. One candidate goes to the judgement `model-2` — `theta`, forgetting of θ: n := min(n; 20) after a pause of the module of no less than 14 days, without a new field, the group `jumps`, without a target, two constants. It was chosen by the exploration of epic E5 — deciding runs of the harness at 2,000 working learners per cell, on all populations — the base and nine variants: forgetting of θ with N 10, 20 and 40 at d₀ 14 and with d₀ 7 and 28 at N 20, the same together with the forgetting numbers of the first round, and a weight of 1/2 for an answer with help. The rule with N = 20 and d₀ = 14 holds all the constraints, is better than the base and shortens catching up after a break. With it the forgetting numbers no longer delay catching up, but none of their sets holds all the constraints: `forget-set` still breaks the error of the estimate and the corridor — most of all outside the `F-` populations, where the rule does not act, but on `F-none` too — and the base of the division of τ alone — both 0.35 and 0.4 — fails one check each. The weight 1/2 for an answer with help is rejected. The judgement `model-2` chose `theta`, and the confirmation passed.** The make-up is forgetting of θ and one run of the weight of an answer with help, without the step of the bands, σ₀, I, ρ and τ₀, which the first round explored and on which there are no new facts; about ten runs with stopping rules, and the tenth, within that budget, the rule of θ with the base 0.4, after `thh20-14` failed one check by a hair.
- **The harness** is `sim` `v0.1.10`, commit ff921be, the tag the gate stood on then ([sim@ff921be](https://github.com/altnovatrail/sim/blob/ff921be/gate.json)): `learner-model` v0.1.6, `engine` v0.1.6, the packages of `modules` v0.2.5 with `olympiad-math` and `grade-3-math` 0.3.0 — with `time` and leading questions — the set `working`, `engine`'s rule, all populations. A run is `sim`'s recipe `just decisive engine <file>`, and a variant is `CANDIDATE_LEARNER_MODEL` with the folder of the variant's working copy: the recipe keeps the build by `gate.json`, and the file names the commit. The base is a run without a candidate, by `sim`'s `go.mod`: SHA-256 `7db81ec7bdf8c02a…92e` — exactly `base` in `internal/criterion/paths.json` of `sim`, so the exploration's base is the same one on which the judgement reads the paths. The variants are commits on top of `v0.1.6` (7d796e2) in a clone in the session's folder; neither they nor the files of the runs go into the repository, and they were never published anywhere, so a repetition assembles them anew by the description below — then the numbers are the same, and the file's header names its own SHA:
  - the rule of θ is in `move` (`step.go`), right after `at := e.PracticeTime`: outside the trial series and for a module with skills, if `pauseDays` from the greatest `LastPractice` of the module's skills to `at` is no less than `thetaPause`, then `AnswerWeight` := min(`AnswerWeight`; `thetaWeight`); both constants are in `model.go`, after `secondsPerDay`. The same as `theta` of the analysis of catching up after a break, but with d₀ and N as constants. N no less than 5 is required: n < 5 would return the module to the trial series (`inTrial`);
  - `thN-d` is the rule with N and d₀: `th10-14` e47c860, `th20-14` 90b6236, `th40-14` 796d781, `th20-7` c7aaba7, `th20-28` 4268e96;
  - `thfs20-14`, 4cd32e6 — `th20-14` and `forget-set`: `stabilityGrowth` 10 and the base of the division of τ 0.35 in `stabilityAfter`; `thh20-14`, 6e36283 — `th20-14` and the base 0.35 alone; `thh040-20-14`, 011f089 — `th20-14` and the base 0.4;
  - `help05`, d98e1d6 — the weight w of an answer with help (`helped`) multiplied by 1/2 in `answers`, so that its pull in the step of θ and δ is half as large, as the variant of the first round's exploration.

  The SHA-256 of the files: `th10-14` — `3103286a394584c8…8e0`, `th20-14` — `356b4969438ce09f…bef`, `th40-14` — `73164447c7dd9367…b5e`, `th20-7` — `77313baeea2d8ad6…df8`, `th20-28` — `fb3651b2c39b706f…3e1`, `thfs20-14` — `329f803c8aeca9fb…3dc`, `thh20-14` — `4994fd294ed902b6…9e9`, `thh040-20-14` — `1ea79fcaa4b28187…623`, `help05` — `1149cd8721bcda8b…801`. A run took from 36 to 41 minutes on 20 cores, and when neighbours shared the host, 50 minutes.
- **The yardstick** is the judgement `sim judge` with the model's criterion by a judgement file in the session's folder: the base and the variants, each with its group and number of constants. At 2,000 learners it reads as a deciding run: "This is a decisive run". But this is the exploration, not the judgement: the judgement `model-2` runs the same working learners with the same seeds, so on a commit with the same code it will repeat their numbers number for number — the file itself differs by the header, which names the commit — and the confirmation on the untouched `held-out-model-1`, which the exploration did not touch, guards against fitting to the working learners. The differences by measures are `sim compare` against the base; below, b − a, with a 95-percent interval.
- **What the runs showed** — the estimate and the group's score with an interval, the "not worse" failures by the judgement's rule; `M3.settle` — answers until catching up on `F-break`, for the base 161.1 on olympiad maths and 336.4 on school maths; the last column is `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths, where the rule costs most:

  "Group" is the score of the group the variant named as its own: the rule of θ — `jumps`, the sets with the forgetting numbers — `forgets`, the weight of help — `learns`; for `thfs20-14` the score in the group `jumps` is +0.008 [−0.010; 0.028] — within the noise. The exploration's judgement of the first stage called `th20-14` and `th40-14` equal to the best, and chose `th20-14`.
- **The price of `th20-14`.** The rule moves numbers only on the six `F-` populations and four numbers on `A-fading`: on the other populations a pause of the module of 14 days does not come, and the files coincide with the base number for number. The corridor `E1.corridor` and the loops `E2` are shifted outside the noise nowhere, so `engine`, which reads the estimate, the uncertainty and "mastered" through `Readout`, pays almost no price. Better are the false "mastered" after a break `M4.false.break` on olympiad maths, by 0.005–0.034 on `F-model`, `F-none`, `F-exp`, `F-growth5` and `F-break`, `M1.rms.200` on `F-break` of olympiad maths, by 0.012, and `M5.caught` on `F-exp` of school maths, by 0.55 answers. Worse are `M6.never` on olympiad maths, by 0.0025–0.0036 on `F-model`, `F-none`, `F-C1`, `F-growth5` and `F-break`: v is wider, and "mastered" comes later — and `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths, by 1.26 [0.19; 2.35] answers at the judgement's tolerance of 2.61. Brier `M2` and `M10.retention` are shifted by less than 0.001 — Brier by less than half of the author's tolerance, 0.002. This is what the analysis of catching up found for `theta` on the `F-` populations of `sim` `v0.1.8`, and on `v0.1.10` the check `M5.caught` holds.
- **Why so.**
  1. The strength N: the smaller N, the faster catching up and the dearer `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths — the answers of a skill after its pause of 14 days or more until the P of the reference task stays within 0.05 of the truth for five answers in a row. Resetting n makes the step of θ after a pause wider, while on `F-none` the truth does not forget and the level does not fall, so a wide step catches up nothing there and only makes P swing around the truth for longer. N 10 fails this check, N 40 hardly moves catching up, N 20 is between them.
  2. The threshold d₀: 7 days catch up a little faster than 14, but fire on ordinary pauses of skills and fail the same check; 28 days are weaker and no cheaper. The threshold of a skill's pause in `sim`'s measures is the same 14 days.
  3. The forgetting numbers: with the rule of θ, catching up for `forget-set` is within the noise of the base, +4.5 [−1.2; 10.0] answers, whereas without it the analysis on `sim` `v0.1.8` saw a delay of 22.2 answers: the delay that the analysis explained by the belief in forgetting with a θ that is not forgotten, the rule removes. But the failures of `forget-set` in the first judgement outside the `F-` populations — `E1.corridor` on G2 and S-adult, `M1.rms.200` on G3-drop and S-scale1.5 — remained: the rule of θ does not act there, so they come from the growth of τ at g 10 itself. Remained too the failure that the analysis expected the rule to remove — `M1.rms.200` on `F-none` of olympiad maths, 0.01196 at 0.01125 — and with it `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths. The base of the division of τ alone breaks the error of the estimate less: at 0.35 — `M1.rms.200` on G8 of olympiad maths, 0.01044 at a tolerance of 0.01, 4% beyond it; at 0.4 G8 holds, 0.0088, but `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths fails, 4.83 at 3.75. This measure on this cell is noisier than the others — the standard error of the difference from 0.6 to 0.9 answers — and at 0.35 it was +1.80, and at 0.4 +3.07: of the two bases neither gives a set that holds everything.
  4. The weight 1/2 for an answer with help: with the leading questions of the packages v0.2.5, help is each answer of an attempt where the button "Hint" gave a question, and the half weight damps these pieces of evidence in the step of θ and δ; what share of the answers they make up, the exploration did not measure. Worst of all is school maths: `M1.rms.200` on G2 is worse by 0.68, `M3.lag` on G2-half by 0.31, catching up on `F-break` by 350 answers. The direction and the strength are the same as in the first round's exploration on packages without leading questions: `learns` there −0.086, here −0.080 — so leading questions do not change the conclusion.
- **What was decided:**
  1. one candidate goes to the judgement `model-2` — `theta`: the rule of θ with N = 20 and d₀ = 14, as the variant `th20-14`, the group `jumps`, no target — the rule does not move the share of those who did not catch up on G3 or the lag on G2-half — two constants, no new field, the state kind stays 1. It is one idea with one set of numbers. The exploration's judgement called `th40-14` equal by the estimate, but of the two chose `th20-14`, and `th40-14` does not shift catching up on `F-break` of olympiad maths outside the noise, while that is the round's target. If the confirmation of `theta` does not pass, the judgement does not try a second candidate on the same held-out learners, so `th40-14` as a second candidate would add no choice;
  2. rejected: `th10-14` and `th20-7` — they fail `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths; `th40-14` and `th20-28` — they hold everything, but are weaker than `th20-14`; `thfs20-14` — seven failures outside catching up; `thh20-14` and `thh040-20-14` — one failure each, and the judgement runs the same learners with the same seeds and will repeat it number for number. The rule of the tolerance — the larger of the author's tolerance and 4.3 standard errors of the difference of this candidate — was recorded before the first candidate and does not change for a candidate, even though the tolerance itself depends on the noise of its difference: for `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths it is from 2.58 to 3.75; `help05` — 62 failures;
  3. Д5 and ρ, τ₀ of Д6 and Д7 were not explored: there were no new facts about them. For the numbers of the start of Д5 such a fact came later, `sim`'s measurement of the fairness by the grade.

— *Why so:* an exploration at the size of the deciding run and on the same harness as the judgement is the lesson of the first round: the coarse look at 300 learners did not see the failures the judgement found. The price is that the judgement `model-2` of `theta` is already known on the working learners, and the confirmation on the held-out ones decides.

— *What the exploration does not decide:* the judgement and the confirmation are `sim`'s. On essays the rule shifted no numbers — the files of `th20-14` and of the base coincide there. A set that keeps the gain of `forget-set` in forgetting without failures outside `F-` — a base between 0.4 and 0.5, or a growth of τ that slows down with τ itself, which [architecture.md](architecture.md) defers in the model of forgetting — is a question for a later round. Epic E7 laid it out on the base with `theta`, and its tasks are deferred, `[?]`, until a candidate of forgetting brings a cause of its own, since `sim` holds no rounds by plan; E7 is in [plan/E7.md](plan/E7.md).

— *Rejected:*
- a candidate for forgetting — a set of the rule of θ with the forgetting numbers — despite the failure by a hair: the judgement will repeat it, and the candidate's exit would give nothing but a run of the judgement;
- more runs on the base of the division of τ — 0.45 and closer to the base: the author allotted about ten runs to the exploration, and the tenth went to the base 0.4, while `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths is so noisy that the exploration cannot find the boundary between 0.4 and 0.5 without several runs per point;
- an exploration on the `F-` populations alone, then a full run for the finalists: the price of the rule outside `F-` for the finalists would be visible too, but a full run of all the variants cost as many hours, and "not worse" was read on all populations at once.

- **The judgement `model-2`** chose `theta`, and the confirmation on `held-out-model-1` passed: on the working learners it repeated the exploration number for number, and on the held-out ones `theta` holds all the constraints and is better than the base; the chosen one came out by the merge of the judgement's task as the release `v0.1.8`. The report `judgements/model-2/report.md` of `sim` at `v0.1.13` ([sim@7c32016](https://github.com/altnovatrail/sim/blob/7c32016/judgements/model-2/report.md)). The deciding runs are 2,000 learners per cell, `sim` after `v0.1.12`, on the candidate's commit a063835:
  - the working learners: the estimate +0.01029 [0.005208; 0.01522], the group `jumps` +0.02052 [0.01775; 0.02325], no "not worse" failures — the same numbers as `th20-14` in the table above. The SHA-256 of the candidate's file is `35051542b9fdaf49…7c19`, the same to the byte as the draft's run on `sim` `v0.1.10`: the run was repeated on another clone and another tag of `sim`;
  - `held-out-model-1`, seed 20261108: the estimate +0.04215 [0.03279; 0.05219], the group `jumps` +0.02025 [0.01765; 0.02294], no failures. The estimate is four times higher than on the working learners, and the score of its own group the same; the confirmation does not require a repetition of the estimate;
  - both sets have the same measures that left the estimate: the path not read or not above zero — `M7.hard` on G1 and S-adult, `M4.false.break` on four `F-` and `M4.false.pages` on three `W-` of school maths — the path within the author's tolerance — three measures of school maths and `M9.hasFalse` on A-unpractised of the pair of maths; the base reads no measures of essays, and on the held-out ones also `M4.false.all` on G0-start0.5 of school maths.

  `sim`'s base, paths and bands are now on the commit of `theta`, and `held-out-model-1` is no longer held out: the next round of the model is confirmed on `held-out-model-2`.

— *Consequence:* the draft `theta` — the rule with N and d₀ as named constants, as the variant `th20-14`, tests and golden files — puts the rule after the check "no answers" in `move`. In the variant the rule stood before it, so a piece of evidence without answers after a pause narrowed n too, while [architecture.md](architecture.md) requires that such a piece of evidence change nothing; `answers` does not read n, so the answers of a piece of evidence with answers are the same. A test holds the edge J = 0 of a piece of evidence whose answers have no primary skills in the graph. The rows Д5–Д7 hold what the exploration learned. — `step.go`, `model.go` and the tests, `testdata/`; `run/DEBT.md`, Д5–Д7; [architecture.md](architecture.md).

## The tag v0.16.3 of `spec`

**R33. `learner-model` stands on `spec` v0.16.3 — the latest release. The tag recorded all six of this repository's rows «в реестр spec», and they are closed. Beyond them it brings Go 1.27.4 in the stage `toolchain` and, in `claudeMdExcludes`, the line that keeps the submodule's rules of parts out of the sessions here — the move takes them — and `spec`'s epic of saving tokens: new rules of RULES.md, the template with `DEBT.md`, `docs/sources.md` and budgets of documents, and the translation of `docs/` into English. That is a task of its own, not the move. The move changes neither code nor schema, and the merge releases the patch `v0.1.7` with the same code and schema — the Dockerfile is in the release's `paths`.** A task outside an epic: the move to the latest release of `spec`.
- **What the tag brings `learner-model`** (`git -C spec diff v0.15.0 v0.16.3` and `spec`'s plan and debt, read 2026-11-13):
  - the answers to the six rows of table 3, all by their recommendations, each recorded by `spec` with a fingerprint: the library's part in moving `sim` to `v0.1.5` is done, and `sim` moved by its tag `v0.1.9`; `sim` raised the model to `v0.1.6`, and `engine` its least version; a limit of 99 items for any task comes in the nearest version of the format that comes out for another reason; `.github`'s shared `go.yml` runs wasip1 on `node:wasi`; `sim` `v0.1.10` pinned the harness before the round's gate; the model's round goes first, and the round of `engine`'s corridor on the base after its judgement;

The pilot is `module-kit`: it moved to v0.16.3 by a pull request of its own, and did the rebuild and the translation as two tasks after it (pull requests #72–#74 of `module-kit`, `main` 85da6d6, read 2026-11-13). With v0.1.33 the job `arm64` of `go.yml` takes the composite action `toolchain`, and every restore of the Go caches falls back on the newest entry of its check and Dockerfile without `go.sum` (sources 5.9). The inputs of the call, the permissions, the lists `.github/coderules.json` and the required checks of the ruleset of `main` do not change.** A task outside an epic: of the debts, only this one could be done before the third round, and the others waited for later epics, new facts or a tag of `sim`.

- rewriting the links of the log and the archives to the list of sources of architecture — the pointer keeps them right;
- translating `sources.md` and `measurements.md` already in the rebuild — the translation is a task of its own, by the brief and with `translation-check`.

— *Consequence:* the documents are outside the release's `paths`: the merge releases no tag. — `.claude/settings.json`, `.devcontainer/devcontainer.json`, AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md, README.md, `.claude/rules/`; RUN.md, `run/`; `docs/`; `docs-budgets.json`.

## A shorter RUN.md

**R36. RUN.md keeps only what is live and what no other file says: the executor's rules, the current epic and the bodies of its tasks not yet done. Tables 2 and 3 are `run/EPICS.md` and `run/QUESTIONS.md`, and RUN.md has no section «Задачи вне эпика»: a task outside an epic is a body of `run/TASKS.md` until done, and then goes to the current epic's archive, section «Задачи вне эпика, сделанные за его время», or with no current epic to the last done one's, «… после него». Rules 6 and 10 repeat CLAUDE.md and RULES.md as a line with a pointer, and rule 10 adds one clause that RULES.md does not say: a commit message or a pull request's description is never a copy of the report, as the template's rule 10 says, while its other words on commits and pull requests are in RULES.md already; rules 3 and 5 are the template's text. The rules keep their numbers. The history of the tables and the section «Задачи вне эпика» went to the archive of the last epic done word for word, not lost. This is the shape `sim` gave its RUN.md.** RUN.md is cleaned of what a session does not need, to spend fewer tokens.
- **What changed** (RUN.md, 37,869 bytes → 18,871 at commit 5d37495, before E7 was unfolded):
  - the opening — without the paragraph on the epics done and without the repeat of "one task is one session";
  - rules 3, 5 and 10 — a pointer to RULES.md; rule 6 — without the list of what `docs-check` checks, which is in CLAUDE.md; rule 2 — the rules of the parts in `.claude/rules/`; rule 9 — a task outside an epic stood in table 1, and without a current epic went to the archive of the last one done; the statuses `[-]` and `[?]` stay the author's decision; the block «Ссылки» — gone, as RULES.md says it;
  - «Статусы», «Кто», «План» and «Тело задачи» — a line each; «Как устроен план: эпики» — the same rules, shorter; the list of done epics — one line each;
  - the list of done epics as it was, the opening paragraphs of tables 2–4 — the history of the epics proposed, of the closed rows of table 3 and of the move of table 4 — and the section «Задачи вне эпика» with the phrases of its three tasks — to [plan/E5.md](plan/E5.md), «Из RUN.md». Table 3 kept its next number, which `docs-check` reads.

— *Why so:* every session reads RUN.md whole, at its start, and half of it was history or a copy of the shared rules that CLAUDE.md already loads. The template of `spec` then kept the section «Задачи вне эпика», but here it held only three finished tasks, whose bodies were in the archive already; a task outside an epic loses nothing by standing in table 1. `sim` made the same choice the same day, and one shape in both repositories is cheaper to read than two.

— *Rejected:*
- keeping the section empty, or creating it with the first task outside an epic — such a task can stand in table 1 like any other;
- deleting the history without moving it, as the first pass of the cleanup did — the archive of the epic is where it would have gone at the next epic's end, and the log links it;
- renumbering the rules — the plan and CLAUDE.md name them by number.

— *Consequence:* a move to a newer tag of `spec` that brings a new RUN.md of the template reads this entry first. The documents are outside the release's `paths`: the merge releases no tag. — RUN.md; `run/`; [plan/E5.md](plan/E5.md).

## Epic E7

**R37. Epic E7 was laid out as the third round of the model's candidates: the forgetting numbers on the base with `theta` — the gate on the latest tag of `sim`, the exploration at the size of the deciding run on that same tag, the drafts of the candidates, the judgement and the summary. Only the gate was done: `sim` holds no judgement of the model's round by plan — it opens a round as an epic once the library brings candidates with their cause, and its next round of the model is the fairness by the grade —, so the exploration, the drafts, the judgement and the summary are deferred, `[?]`, until a candidate of forgetting brings a cause of its own, and E7 with its gate is in [plan/E7.md](plan/E7.md). The exploration stopped with its variants built and no run made. Each task is a branch of its own from `main`, which the executor takes to the merge, except the drafts of the candidates.** The make-up is by the row of E7 in the epics for later, which the summary of E5 proposed; a branch per task, as in E4 and in E5 from its draft on.
- **What was found** (2026-11-13; `sim` `main` 9c8f782 and `engine` `main`, through `gh api`):
  - `sim`'s base is the release `v0.1.8`, commit ab688b2, at the tag `v0.1.14`, and its E5 waits only for its summary. `sim` names the next round of the model — `judgements/model-3/`, confirmed on `held-out-model-2` — but no task of `sim` judges it yet, and `sim`'s epics for later hold only the judgement `engine-2`;
  - `engine` is in its epic "Lesson consolidation": a task of it waits for a tag of `sim` with the step and the criterion that `engine` gave `sim`, and the next for a judgement of `sim`. The candidates of `engine`'s second round of the corridor, which `engine-2` judges, do not exist yet;
  - "Gate" here stands on `sim` `v0.1.13`, commit 7c32016, whose base is the commit of `theta` a063835; `v0.1.14` has the same base on the release;
  - the growth of τ is τ · (1 + g · (1 − R)) for a right answer and halving for a wrong one (`stabilityAfter`, `model.go`); in FSRS the gain of stability falls with stability (sources 3.1). A growth that depends on τ itself needs no new field: τ is in the state, and the state kind stays 1.
- **What was decided:**
  1. five tasks: the gate on the latest tag of `sim`; the exploration — the base of the division of τ between 0.4 and 0.5 and a growth of τ that slows down with τ, alone and with g, measured on the checks that the sets of E5's exploration failed; the drafts, the judgement and the release, the summary — as in E5. The judgement was to be `model-3` with its confirmation on `held-out-model-2`, and there is no such judgement in `sim`'s queue;
  2. no analysis task as in E5: E5's exploration already names the cause of the failures of `forget-set` outside `F-` — the growth of τ at g 10, on which the rule of θ does not act;
  3. a branch per task from `main`, a pull request and a merge that the executor takes to green CI itself, as in E5; the drafts of the candidates are branches from `main` and wait for the judgement, and the judgement's task merges the chosen one, which releases a patch of the line `0.1`. This holds for E8 too, but for the merge in a run of the orchestrator, which the orchestrator does;
  4. the tag of `sim` was to be held from the gate to the judgement's choice, since the numbers of the exploration equal the judgement's on the working learners only on the same tag; with no judgement, nothing holds it. Who holds `sim`'s pin first — this round, `engine`'s lesson consolidation or `engine-2` — went to `spec` «в реестр spec», and its answer is the deferral above.

— *Why so:* the gate before the exploration is the order of E4 and E5: a red "Gate" on a candidate must mean a shift of the measure, not a new tag. The exploration at the size of the deciding run on the judgement's tag is the lesson of the first round and gave the judgement `model-2` number for number.

— *Rejected:*
- an analysis task as in E5 — the cause is known, and an analysis would add no fact the exploration needs;
- one branch of the epic, as E5 went until its draft — the author chose a branch per task; the code of the round is only the drafts, and one of them goes into `main`, so the round comes out as one release either way;
- the exploration on `sim` `v0.1.13`, without the gate — the judgement goes on the latest tag, and a later tag would shift the exploration's numbers;
- Д5, ρ, τ₀ and the weight of an answer with help in the round — there are no new facts about them.

— *Consequence:* the documents are outside the release's `paths`: the merge releases no tag. — RUN.md; `run/EPICS.md`, `run/QUESTIONS.md`, `run/DEBT.md`, Д6; [architecture.md](architecture.md); [plan/E7.md](plan/E7.md).

## Epic E8

**R40. Epic E8 was the round of fairness by the grade: why the model leaves a start above the truth more slowly than it rises from a start below it, then candidates that bring `M11.same` of `D-grade5` and `D-grade1` up to 0.90 under the model's criterion as is, judged by `sim` on the candidate's commit, and the chosen one released or the exit recorded, and the outcome «в реестр spec» for `spec`'s DPIA — its impact assessment of profiling and its conditions before the first child. The analysis found the cause in the trial series and the slow step after it, and the exploration found no candidate; the long runs of fitting constants on `sim` stop until there are 100 children, so the drafts and the judgement are deferred, `[?]`, and the round ended with its exit. Its outcome went to `spec` with the exploration's numbers: the residual risk is accepted in the DPIA's sign-off, in its version 24, and the first child waits for three rules in place of calibration, built here and checked by a short run of `sim`; the start moves in the model, a band below the band of the grade, and `engine` does not change. E7's tasks of forgetting are deferred, `[?]`, and E7 is in its archive with its gate.** A task outside an epic laid it out, written by the text that `spec`'s orchestrator gave for the round in its plan of E14. The split into tasks is the executor's: in a run of the orchestrator nobody approves a plan, and its questions are rows of table 3.
- **What was found** (2026-11-16; `sim` `main` c5e9a4c, through `gh api` and a clone outside the repository):
  - the evaluation `fairness-1` of `sim`, on the base `learner-model` v0.1.8 and `engine` v0.1.6: under grades 1 and 5 of olympiad maths, where the model starts a band below and a band above the one truth, the estimate comes to that under grade 3 by the 50th answer for 0.812 [0.795; 0.829] and 0.5525 [0.53; 0.5735] of the learners, against the bound 0.90, after 32.5 and 83.3 answers on average, a learner who never comes within the run of 200 answers counting as 200; the bands of the tasks hold. "Why the start above the truth is left more slowly is not measured here; it is a question for `learner-model`", says `sim`'s log. `fair` holds a candidate no worse than the base but does not reward a better one;
  - `sim` holds no rounds by plan, and `model-3` has left its queue; the one judgement in it is this round, an epic of `sim`'s laid out once `learner-model` takes the round, with its criterion written down before the first candidate — the model's criterion as is, the target `M11.same` of `D-grade1` and `D-grade5`, its own folder and held-out set, as for `engine-lesson-1`. Its latest tag is `v0.1.21`, with the bits of `v0.1.20`; "Gate" here is on `v0.1.14`;
  - the start is θ₀, the offset of the grade's band; while the weight of answers is below 5, θ is the maximum of the trial series' posterior with the prior N(θ₀, σ₀²), σ₀ = 2.5; then the step of Elo with K_θ = max(0.2 / (1 + 0.05 · n), 0.05). These are numbers a candidate changes without a new field. A start other than the grade's band changes `spec`'s rule "A learner of a grade starts in the band of their grade" and its concept of the learner model;
  - σ₀ 3.0 was explored in E3, at 300 learners and before `M11` existed: no better than the base on the overall score, with 28 constraints violated, but `off-band` — the learners whose grade is off their level — up by +0.062 beyond the noise, while σ₀ 2.0 worsened it by −0.217 (Д5).
- **What was decided:**
  1. the tasks: the analysis of the asymmetry, with a measurement, as in E5; the gate on the tag of `sim` on which the judgement's criterion is written, as in E7; the exploration at the size of the deciding run on that tag; the drafts; the judgement and the release; the outcome «в реестр spec» for the DPIA, the summary and the archive. The analysis and the gate do not wait for each other, and run one at a time, in either order, as tasks of table 1 do; the exploration waits for both. The exploration ended in the round's exit, so the drafts and the judgement are `[?]`, and the outcome carried its numbers to the DPIA, as this decision has it for an exit;
  2. the judgement waits for `sim`'s judgement of the candidate's commit, by `CANDIDATE_LEARNER_MODEL`, not for a release of `sim`; the tag of `sim` with the chosen one's bands is needed only by its merge. It waits with the judgement;
  3. a candidate with a start by the grade other than the grade's band is also a row «в реестр spec», written in the exploration before its draft, and the judgement merges it only after `spec`'s answer. It waits with the judgement;
  4. a candidate changes numbers or a rule without a new field; a new field — state kind 2 and a recomputation of `api`'s cache — only by the author's answer to a row of table 3 in the planning of the drafts. It waits with the drafts;
  5. branches as in E7: a branch and a pull request per task, the drafts of the candidates as draft pull requests; in a run of the orchestrator the orchestrator merges, not the executor;
  6. E7's tasks of forgetting — its exploration, drafts, judgement and summary — are `[?]`, each with its reason and when to return: `sim` holds no judgement `model-3`; back when a candidate of forgetting brings a cause of its own — a fault that the gate or a measurement found, a need of the product, a row of `spec`. Its exploration stays where it stopped — the variants of stage 1 built outside the repository, no run made. No candidate of forgetting brought a cause of its own while E8 went;

— *Why so:* the evaluation leaves the "why" to this repository, and without it the exploration would guess which numbers to move, as the first round did before its analysis found the cause of its failure. The gate before the exploration and the exploration on the judgement's tag are the lessons of E4, E5 and E7. The outcome is a task of its own: the first child waits for it, by the DPIA's conditions before the first child, and it must come out on an exit too, when the judgement releases nothing.

— *Rejected:*

- the exploration without an analysis — `sim`'s evaluation leaves the asymmetry unexplained, and `sim` no longer judges a candidate without a cause;
- the exploration on `sim` `v0.1.14` or `v0.1.21`, without the gate — the judgement goes on the tag of its criterion, and another tag would make a red "Gate" ambiguous;
- the outcome inside the judgement — on an exit the judgement has nothing to merge, and the row for the DPIA would wait on a pull request.
