## The analysis of E8

**R41. Under grade 5 the estimate comes to grade 3's later on average because of a tail, not because every learner comes slowly: 0.314 of the learners never come within the run of 200 answers, against 0.021 under grade 1, and those who come do so in 29.9 answers on average, against 28.9. The tail starts in the trial series. The engine gives its tasks a band higher under grade 5, as the model's start is, and the maximum of the series' posterior moves with the start and the tasks alike: a learner who answers the harder tasks as under grade 3 ends the series up to as far above grade 3's estimate as it started — 2.5 to the digit where its tasks were grade 3's a band up. Under grade 1 the module has no band below the first, the tasks of the series are grade 3's, and only the prior differs. After the series the step of θ — K_θ falling to 0.05, with δ taking much of each pull — closes such a gap slowly. σ₀ moves grade 1 alone; the step after the series and a longer series move grade 5, and at the base's three cells no set of them brings grade 5 to 0.90. Neither guessing nor forgetting is the cause of the tail.** The analysis of epic E8; its plan is the executor's, in a run of `spec`'s orchestrator.
- **The harness** is `sim` `v0.1.21`, commit bc9aaaf, whose bench `v0.1.22` keeps ([sim, bc9aaaf to 38f687c](https://github.com/altnovatrail/sim/compare/bc9aaaf...38f687c)), with the base of its `go.mod` — `learner-model` v0.1.8, `engine` v0.1.6 —, the cells `D-grade1`, `D-grade3` and `D-grade5` at 2,000 working learners each, and a trace of θ, P, P* and the task after each of the first 200 answers, built in the clone in the session's folder; the variants are commits on top of `main` e3aaee6 there. How to repeat the runs, the variants and the SHA-256 of every file are measurements 5.1. On the base the trace gives `M11` of `sim`'s evaluation `fairness-1` to every digit, so the cells on `v0.1.21` are its own. 12 runs of the three cells — one `sim run`, the base's trace and ten variants' — took from 2 to about 8 minutes each by the clock on 20 cores, the longer ones while the machine was busy with other work.
- **What the runs showed** (measurements 5.2, 5.3):
  1. *a tail.* The learners who never come carry 62.8 of grade 5's 83.33 answers, and 4.2 of grade 1's 32.52; the median is 30 answers under grade 5 and 16 under grade 1. Against the truth grade 5 is no worse than grade 3: |θ − θ*| after the 50th answer is 0.59 against 0.64, after the 200th 0.37 against 0.41 — but it is high, by +0.25 and +0.10, while grade 3 is at −0.04 and −0.07 and grade 1 at −0.41 and −0.30. `M11` measures whether the two estimates agree, and under grade 5 they disagree in both directions;
  2. *the trial series.* Its five tasks — one skill's difficulties 1, 3 and 5, then the next skill's 1 and 3 — are grade 3's under grade 1 for every learner, while under grade 5 the first skill is of band 2, not band 1, for every learner. The learner n answers by the same fractions under every grade, as `sim`'s cells `D-` are made, so under grade 5, on harder tasks, nobody has fewer wrong answers. Where the counts are equal — 0.201 of the learners — the series ends 1.7–2.5 above grade 3's estimate, and 0.958 of them never come; 288 learners whose five tasks were exactly the reference's a band up and who answered them alike end it at the reference plus 2.5 to every digit of the trace. Where grade 5 has two or three wrong answers and grade 3 none — 0.106 of the learners —, the series ends 1.4–3.1 below the reference, which stays high after five right answers on easy tasks, and 0.72 of them never come. Under grade 1 the same five answers end the series 0.42–0.80 below the reference;
  3. *after the series.* A gap of 2.5 is 1.5 after the 50th answer and 0.9 after the 200th. The step is K_θ · (S − P), K_θ = 0.2 / (1 + 0.05 · n) down to the floor 0.05 at n = 60, and the skills' δ, with K_δ = 0.4 / (1 + 0.05 · m) and m counted by skill, take much of each pull: under grade 5 the mean δ of the tasks' skills is −0.21 over answers 101–200 against the reference's −0.11. Without the step of δ — a diagnostic, K₀ of δ 0 — 0.180 of grade 5 never come instead of 0.314;
  4. *the variants.* σ₀ 3.0 brings grade 1 to 0.9135 and leaves grade 5 at 0.5725; σ₀ 2.0 and 4.0 are worse under both grades, 4.0 because θ in the series swings far enough for the engine to give grade 1 other tasks than grade 3. Under grade 5 the share of equal-series learners who never come stays 0.948–0.970 at σ₀ 2.0, 2.5 and 3.0: with the tasks moved by the start, a prior of any width moves with them. A series of weight 10, a floor of K_θ of 0.1 and K₀ of θ 0.4 bring grade 5's never to 0.188, 0.144 and 0.125 and its `M11.same` to 0.5945, 0.5655 and 0.669. σ₀ 3.0 with K₀ 0.4 and a series of 10 gives 0.936 under grade 1 and 0.735 under grade 5, never 0.073 — the most of the runs. The step without forgetting moves grade 5 a little — never 0.285 against 0.314 — and grade 1 the other way, `M11.same` 0.7945 against 0.812, and 0.928 of grade 5's equal-series learners still never come.
- **Why so.** In the series θ is the maximum of L(θ) = −(θ − θ₀)² / (2σ₀²) + Σ log-likelihood of the answers. When θ₀ and every βᵢ move by 2.5 and the outcomes stay, L moves by 2.5 and its maximum with it: the series cannot tell a start a band too high from the truth unless the answers differ. They differ only by the tasks being harder for the learner, and on three to five tasks one learner in five answers them as under the true band. The engine chooses the series' tasks by the model's P — a skill is within reach when a task of it has P ≥ 0.70, the corridor is 0.70–0.85 ([`skills.go` of `engine` v0.1.6](https://github.com/altnovatrail/engine/blob/57e0ab8/skills.go)) —, so the tasks follow the start; under grade 1 they cannot go below the first band, which is where grade 3's already are. After the series the gap is left to the step of Elo, whose K_θ is at most 0.16 at n = 5 and falls to the floor by n = 60, while each new skill's δ starts at 0 and takes the larger share of the pull. Grade 1's tail is the mirror of the same with the reference: a learner high above grade 3's start answers all of the easy series right, the likelihood of right answers has no maximum, and the prior keeps 0.80 of the 2.5 between the starts, against 0.42–0.51 after one or two wrong answers.
- **What does not explain it:**
  1. the guessing floor: no item of the series has guessing, and 20 % of the answers after it are a choice of five, alike under the grades;
  2. forgetting in the step: without it the tail of grade 5 is 0.285 instead of 0.314, and 0.928 of its equal-series learners still never come; it is no cause of the tail, though its share of the step after the series is for the exploration to weigh with K₀;
  3. σ₀ alone: it reaches grade 1, whose tasks are the reference's, but not the equal-series learners of grade 5;
  4. the bands of the tasks after the 50th answer: `M11.band` holds in `sim`'s evaluation.
- **What was decided:**
  1. the cause is the trial series with tasks that move with the start, which leaves one learner in five a band off, and the slow step of θ after it. Both are the model's to shorten, and the first is also the engine's choice: a row «в реестр spec» carried it to `engine` and `sim`, and `spec`'s answer is the start of the estimate a band below the band of the grade, in this library, not the tasks of `engine`'s trial series, which would leave the model's start where the asymmetry is; the round went on with what the model can;
  2. what went into the exploration: the numbers of the step after the series — K₀ of θ (0.3–0.4) and its floor (0.075–0.1) —, the weight of the series (7.5–10), σ₀ 3.0 only together with them, and K₀ of δ below 0.4 as a lead; their pairs and the triple of measurements 5.3 as the first sets. Beside `M11.same`, `M11.answers` and `M11.band`, the exploration reads the share that never comes and |θ − θ*| of all three cells — a set that brings the grades together by moving grade 3 off the truth is no fairer —, and the price of a wider step on the 65 other cells, which these runs did not measure (measurements 5.3);
  3. a start other than the grade's band is not measured here. The truth of the cells `D-` is on the middle band, so a start drawn toward the middle of the scale wins `M11` by construction, and such a rule is measured on the learners whose truth is off the middle band too before its row «в реестр spec»;
  4. the risk of the exploration is sharper: at the base's three cells the most of these runs is 0.735 under grade 5, and what is left is the learners whose series repeats the true band's answers. The risk came true: at the size of the deciding run each of these sets lifts `M11.same` but K₀ of δ alone, which moves it within the noise, none of those checked holds every constraint, and grade 5 stays below 0.90 in all 48 sets screened — at most 0.853; the round ended with its exit, and its long runs stopped.
- **Rejected:**
  - σ₀ alone as the exploration's set: it does not reach the cause under grade 5, and 2.0 and 4.0 are worse under both grades;
  - the step without forgetting and K₀ of δ 0 as candidates: the first barely moves `M11` and leaves the tail, and the second removes the skills' corrections, which the model keeps;
  - runs of the other 65 cells here: the cause is in the three cells, and the price of the sets is the exploration's, at the size of the deciding run;
  - a trace of the engine's choice: the tasks the trace shows told the causes apart without it.

— *Consequence:* nothing changes in the code and the tests. — [measurements.md](measurements.md); `docs/decisions.md`; [architecture.md](architecture.md); RUN.md; [sources.md](sources.md); [run/DEBT.md](../run/DEBT.md), Д5; [run/QUESTIONS.md](../run/QUESTIONS.md); [plan/E8.md](plan/E8.md).

## The exploration of E8

**R42. The exploration of E8 found no candidate for the judgement `model-fair-1`: every set of the model's numbers of the start and the step that was checked against "no worse" — 12 of the 48 screened, the strongest and the most promising of each kind — lifts `M11.same` of grades 1 and 5 and fails "no worse" elsewhere, and none brings grade 5 near the bound 0.90 — at the three cells `D-` the most of 48 sets is 0.853, and the strongest set run in full, `kb06n50w10`, fails 23 checks. The threshold 0.90 is not reached by fitting constants. The long runs of fitting constants on `sim` stop until there are 100 children: the drafts and the judgement are deferred, `[?]`, and the round went to its outcome with the numbers of this exploration. The base stays as it is.** The exploration of epic E8, its plan the executor's in a run of `spec`'s orchestrator. Its budget was about ten decisive runs, the base first and a screening on the cells `D-` before them, since the analysis found two causes. The stop is the platform's, in `spec`'s log: no more long runs of fitting constants on `sim` while there are fewer than 100 children; the fast gate in CI stays, and so do one-off analyses of how the model works and rules in place of calibration. Of these rules the start and the guard are this library's — the estimate of a new learner starts a band below the band of the grade, and three failures in a row of a module lower its level by a band —, and the adult's correction is a fact of the evidence log that `api` appends, a band in one step; building them is a task of its own here. Rejected with the stop: carrying the round E8 to the bound 0.90.
- **The harness** is `sim` `v0.1.23`, commit 61ed988, the tag of "Gate" and of the criterion `model-fair-1` ([sim@61ed988](https://github.com/altnovatrail/sim/tree/61ed988)), with the base of its `go.mod`, `learner-model` v0.1.8 and `engine` v0.1.6. The cells `D-` of the base give the bits of the analysis — the SHA-256 of measurements 5.1 —, and its decisive run gives `base` of `sim`'s `paths.json` to the byte. The yardstick is `sim judge` by a judgement file of `model-fair-1` in the session's folder: the target, `fair` and every "no worse" of the model's criterion as is. How to repeat the runs, the variants, their commits and the SHA-256 of the files are measurements 7; the variants are commits on top of `main` 26be23c in a clone in the session's folder and went nowhere.
- **The runs:** 48 sets screened on the three cells `D-` at 2,000 working learners; 9 sets on the twelve cells of risk, judged in a copy of the base's file; 5 decisive runs — the base, `k04w10`, `kb04n50w10`, `kb06n50w10` and `k04d02` —, within the budget of about ten. Two more were stopped unfinished, and one run of the cells of risk was not read, when the stopping rules or the stop of long runs came first (measurements 7.3, 7.4).
- **What the runs showed** (measurements 7.2–7.5):
  1. *the target moves.* K₀ of θ, the weight of the series and σ₀, alone or together, lift `M11.same` of both grades: K₀ of θ 0.4 and a series of 10, +0.124 [0.109; 0.139]; with K₀ 0.8, +0.205 and grade 5 at 0.853. The floor of K_θ moves the share that never comes, not `M11.same`, which reads the first 50 answers; a K_θ that decays faster, so as to be larger only right after the series, moves nothing or less — the gap is closed between n = 20 and n = 50. σ₀ 3.0 brings grade 1 to 0.91 and leaves grade 5 at 0.57, as the analysis found. None of the sets read moves grade 3 off the truth: its `M1` after the 200th answer is no worse than the base's (measurements 7.2);
  2. *the price moves with it.* Each of the four decisive candidates is better than the base by the target and fails 10 to 23 checks "no worse": `k04w10` 13, `kb04n50w10` 10, `kb06n50w10` 23, `k04d02` 18. A wider step of θ, even only below n = 50, breaks `M1.rms.200` on the cells of school maths and on `W-repeat` by up to 0.33 at the tolerance 0.01; at the cells of risk alone a series of 10 fails 6 checks, the start of `S-adult` among them, and σ₀ 3.0 fails 17, the error of the estimate and Brier;
  3. *`W-repeat`.* There the base's estimate is already +2.72 above the truth after the 200th answer: a repeat of a task whose solution has gone to the device weighs in full (Д7), and any wider step of θ adds to it. Moving the pull from δ to θ — K₀ of θ 0.4 and of δ 0.2, the sum 0.6 of the base — holds `W-repeat`, and the model's score is better than the base's, +0.051 [0.039; 0.062]; but δ that learns more slowly then breaks "mastered" late, `M6.never`, on five cells, the error on `G5`, `G0-skills1` and `S-scale3.5`, and the review dates `E3` on four cells of forgetting — 18 failures. A smaller sum breaks the jumps `G3` and `G3-drop`; a larger share of θ, 0.5 with 0.1, breaks `M6.never` and the start of `S-adult` on the cells of risk alone;
  4. *the bound.* Grade 1 passes 0.90 with K₀ of θ 0.5 and up at the base's decay of K_θ, or with σ₀ 3.0; grade 5 does not in any set, and the sets that come nearest are those that fail most. What the analysis left — one learner in five whose series repeats the true band's answers on tasks a band up — is the engine's choice as much as the model's.
- **What was decided:**
  1. no candidate goes to `model-fair-1`, and the drafts have nothing to draft: by the exploration's risks, the round's exit. The stop of long runs makes it so without a row of table 3: the drafts and the judgement are `[?]` until 100 children, and the outcome, which `spec`'s coordinator starts, carries the numbers of this exploration to the DPIA's impact assessment of profiling and its conditions before the first child;
  2. the threshold 0.90 of `sim`'s criterion is not reached by fitting the model's constants: at the cells `D-` grade 5 stays below it in all 48 sets, and every set that lifts it and was checked is paid for on the 65 other cells; the 36 sets not checked lie between those checked or beyond the strongest of them, so no unchecked set is likely to hold where its neighbours do not. The levers that are left are the rules in place of calibration — the estimate starting from below, an adult's correction of the level, a guard after failures in a row — and the engine's choice of the trial series' tasks. Which of them this library takes is `spec`'s, above, and building them is a task of its own here, not of this round;
  3. the facts worth keeping for whoever returns: the gap after the series is closed between n = 20 and n = 50, so a faster decay of K_θ is no lever; a wider step of θ meets the full weight of a repeat on `W-repeat` (Д7) and needs a smaller step of δ, which in turn delays "mastered"; a copy of the base's decisive file with a variant's lines on the cells of risk is a cheap and exact screening of "no worse" there, but no more — `k04d02` held them all but four and failed eighteen on the decisive run.
- **Rejected:**
  - more decisive runs — the stop of long runs, and the stopping rules too: the sets left to measure are milder than `k04d02` or stronger than `kb06n50w10`, and the first lift less while the second fail more;
  - a start by the grade other than the grade's band: not measured. `spec` keeps a learner in the band of their grade, a start drawn toward the middle wins `M11` on the cells `D-` by construction, and the rule "the estimate starts from below" was `spec`'s to write first;
  - a candidate with failures, as the best that can be had: the judgement's constraints are written down before the first candidate ([sim@61ed988](https://github.com/altnovatrail/sim/blob/61ed988/docs/decisions.md)), and the judgement would repeat each failure number for number on the same learners;
  - a rule for the weight of a repeat as part of this round: it is the weights' own question (Д7), with its own cause, and its first variant, the weight 1/2 of an answer with help, failed 62 checks.

— *Consequence:* nothing changes in the code and the tests, and the documents are outside the release's `paths`: the merge releases no tag. — [measurements.md](measurements.md); `docs/decisions.md`; RUN.md; [architecture.md](architecture.md); [run/DEBT.md](../run/DEBT.md), Д5 and Д7; [run/QUESTIONS.md](../run/QUESTIONS.md); [plan/E8.md](plan/E8.md).

## The speed of CI and the tag v0.18.4 of `spec`

**R43. CI is faster, and what its checks prove does not change. `ci-test` runs the tests twice, each a check of its own: `go test -race` and then the run of the coverage with `-covermode=set`. "Gate" is four jobs "Gate / part k", each `sim`'s `just gate-part k 4`, and after them the check "Gate", `just gate-check bands`, on `ubuntu-24.04-arm`, with `sim`'s cache of Go, on `sim` `v0.1.25`. The shared workflows are `.github` v0.1.33, and "Proto" takes the image by the restore and the script of the image steps of its composite action `toolchain` word for word, but `SAVE`, which stays `"false"`. `spec` was raised to v0.18.4, and `.devcontainer/Dockerfile` has the stage `toolchain-core`, which `toolchain` adds nothing to; from `spec` v0.18.6 on, `.devcontainer/` is the output of its template, whose Dockerfile's comments name the stage `toolchain-core` and whose `devcontainer.json` has `"shutdownAction": "none"`.** The executor's, in a run «Сделай эпик E…» of `spec`'s orchestrator, by `spec`'s plan of the speed of CI, which leaves a change of what a check proves to the repository; the releases that plan rests on — `.github` v0.1.31 and `spec` v0.18.4 — were out, so the change goes whole, with the move to both, and the change of `.devcontainer/` has the author's consent. How it is built is the executor's: no document reserves it for the author.
- **What changes:**
  - `justfile`, `ci-test` — `go test -race -count=1 ./...`, then `go test -count=1 -covermode=set -coverpkg=./... -coverprofile=coverage/go.out ./...`, in place of one run with `-race` and `-covermode=atomic`. Go takes `atomic` by itself under `-race`, "significantly more expensive", and `set` says whether a statement ran (sources 5.8), which is all `codecov/patch` reads. Both still run in "Go / test" and "Go / arm64";
  - "Gate" — four jobs "Gate / part k" and the check "Gate", by the jobs of `engine`'s gate and `sim`'s recipes ([sim@0a31313](https://github.com/altnovatrail/sim/blob/0a31313/justfile)): each part writes its share of the learners into a folder that goes on as an artifact, and "Gate" merges the four, holds the build to `gate.json` and the numbers to their bands, with the same table and exit as `just gate`, and refuses a part missing, repeated or of another run. "Gate" runs when a part failed too, `!cancelled()`, and then fails. Each part checks the tag against the SHA first, by the step "sim tag", so a wrong pair stops in seconds; `SIM_TAG` and `SIM_COMMIT` move to the `env` of the workflow, named once for all five jobs. All five run on `ubuntu-24.04-arm`, since a part splits its share by the runner's processors. The cache of Go — `GOCACHE` and `GOMODCACHE` — is the steps of `sim`'s `gate.yml` word for word: keyed by `sim/go.sum` and `sim/.devcontainer/Dockerfile`, with `restore-keys`, and part 0 saves it on a push to `main` alone; its builds are touched after the restore, as `go.yml` touches its own, so that Go does not delete a build of an entry older than five days at the end of the first command. The image of `sim`'s stage comes by the steps of the job `arm64` of `go.yml` v0.1.31, keyed by `sim`'s Dockerfile, saved by part 0 on a push to `main`. The limit is 15 minutes a job, as in `sim`'s `gate.yml`;
  - `sim` `v0.1.25`, commit 0a31313, in place of `v0.1.23`: the latest tag and the first with `gate.yml` for callers. `gate.json`, `go.sum` and `.devcontainer/` are those of `v0.1.23` ([sim, 61ed988 to 0a31313](https://github.com/altnovatrail/sim/compare/61ed988...0a31313)), so the base — `learner-model` v0.1.8 at ab688b2 and `engine` v0.1.6 —, the 69 bands and the image are the same, and so is what a candidate's gate means;
  - `.github/workflows/` — the seven references from fab102d `# v0.1.24` to 93c848f `# v0.1.31` (sources 5.8): `go.yml` takes the caches of Go, builds the image on a miss in one job and keeps it in one file that only a push to `main` saves, and runs the targets as a matrix and those of `fma-diff` at once; `release.yml` builds the stage `toolchain-core` and signs its files at once; `codeql.yml` takes Go from the archive the Dockerfile pins, with no image. "Proto" takes the image by the three steps of the job `arm64` of that `go.yml` word for word, `STAGES: toolchain`, on its amd64 runner: the entry is the one `go.yml` fills there, and "Proto" saves none, since `go.yml`'s job `toolchain` saves it on a push to `main`. It takes no cache of Go: buf, tar and gzip are all it runs. With `.github` v0.1.33 the job `arm64` of `go.yml` has no steps of the image of its own and takes the composite action `toolchain` (sources 5.9), so "Proto" takes the restore and the script of that action's image steps word for word, `SAVE` `"false"`, and neither the action itself nor a cache of Go;
  - `spec/` — at v0.18.4, commit 5867a6c, the latest release (`gh release verify v0.18.4 -R altnovatrail/spec`). The tag brings the stage `toolchain-core` of the template, apt from the snapshot alone — the kind `go` installs nothing by apt —, the tests of `tools/` in parts and the rows of the speed of CI in `spec`'s plan. `.devcontainer/Dockerfile` is the output of `just new-repo-draft learner-model go 3.8 v0.18.4` in a clone of `spec` v0.18.4 in the session's folder, byte for byte: the stage `toolchain-core` holds what `toolchain` held — Node, just, Go and buf —, and `toolchain` is built `FROM toolchain-core` and adds nothing. Nothing has to move into the core for the release or the checks, as `.github` asks a caller to check with the move: the stage installs the four alone (sources 13.8 `.github`), `release-files` runs tar and gzip of the base image, and `ci-lint`, `ci-vuln`, `ci-licenses` and `ci-proto` run Go and buf. The rest of `.devcontainer/`, `.claude/settings.json` and AGENTS.md are the output's already (`diff -r`). The tag is in CLAUDE.md, README.md, the start of RUN.md and the status of [architecture.md](architecture.md).
- **Checked** (2026-11-16, in the container, 20 cores): `just ci-test` green, the profile `mode: set`, and on the same code the profile of the old run and of the new mark the same 2,260 blocks as run, 99.1 % of the statements; `just ci-lint` green. `sim`'s `just gate-part k 4` for k = 0…3 from a clone at 0a31313, `GATE_LEARNER_MODEL` this checkout, then `just gate-check bands` — "The gate is green" — and `just gate-check exact` — "every number is the recorded ones to the last bit". The times and CI of the pull request are measurements 8.

— *Why:* the gate was the longest path of CI, and `-race` with `-covermode=atomic` in one run four times the two apart (measurements 3.1 spec; measurements 8). What the gate proves does not change: a learner comes from its seeds alone, `sim` merges the parts by its own check, and `sim`'s CI holds amd64 and arm64 to the same bits ([sim@0a31313](https://github.com/altnovatrail/sim/blob/0a31313/docs/decisions.md)). `spec`'s rule for the gate names `ubuntu-24.04` for its job; its plan of the speed of CI moves it to arm64 for the same bits, as it did for `engine`, and leaves such a change to the repository. The cache of Go, rejected as giving "almost nothing" when the gate was one job ([sim@09e5a84](https://github.com/altnovatrail/sim/blob/09e5a84/RUN.md)), now spares five jobs the build of the bench, and it is `sim`'s own steps, not a key of this repository's. The move to v0.18.4 is what a release of `.github` v0.1.31 needs to build `toolchain-core` alone: without the stage it builds `toolchain`, with a notice.

— *Rejected:*
- `sim`'s `gate.yml` called by its tag — its check is "Gate / <name>", not "Gate", so the ruleset of `main` would change, a setting outside the repository that waits for the author's confirmation, or a job "Gate" after it would add a job to the path; and the gate stays a job of the caller's own, as `engine` keeps it;
- `-race` on part of the packages: all three together take about 13 s with it (measurements 8), so there is nothing to win;
- the two runs of `ci-test` at once: the runner's processors are what both runs use, and two outputs at once would mix in the log;
- the gate on amd64 or on both: amd64 is slower by about a minute, and both would repeat what `sim`'s CI holds;
- "Proto" in `toolchain-core`: the two stages hold the same here, and `go.yml` keeps one entry of `toolchain` for every job, which "Proto" shares.

— *Consequence:* the container takes the new stages with "Rebuild Container" after the merge — the author's step; what the devcontainer holds does not change. `.devcontainer/Dockerfile`, `justfile` and `.github/workflows/release.yml` are in the release's `paths`, so the merge releases the next patch with the same code and schema, built in `toolchain-core`. The hash of the Dockerfile changes, so the first push to `main` builds the image and saves new entries; until then a pull request misses them. The ruleset of `main` does not change: "Gate" keeps its name, and the parts are no required check. — `justfile`; `.github/workflows/`; `.devcontainer/`; `spec/`; CLAUDE.md, README.md, RUN.md; [architecture.md](architecture.md); [.claude/rules/ci.md](../.claude/rules/ci.md); [sources.md](sources.md); [measurements.md](measurements.md); `docs/decisions.md`; [plan/E8.md](plan/E8.md).

- **Checked** (2026-11-16, in the container): `just -f spec/justfile docs-check-repo` — ok; `just ci-lint` — green, `GOPLS_VERSION` of the Dockerfile and the lock file `.devcontainer/gopls/` agree. The code and the schema do not change, so the checks of the parts `go` and `proto` do not apply.

— *Why one task:* the move is a submodule, two files of the container and the record of two answers, within one review; a move larger than that is split, as the move to v0.18.3 was for the plan's layout.

— *Why the rules are not here:* they change what the model computes, so they go through the gate of `sim` and its short run by a criterion written before it, and `spec`'s plan gave them a row of their own, which the orchestrator ran here after this move. Until then Д20 of table 4 named them, so that this repository's plan named them too.

— *Why the README is so:* the status and the tag in it go stale with each task and each move, the license is named by `LICENSE`, `NOTICE` and the badge, and a command or an index of documents is for whoever works on the repository, which `docs/README.md` is for. Nothing of the status is lost: what the library does is CLAUDE.md, "The component", and the opening of README.md; the gate's tag of `sim` is this log and [architecture.md](architecture.md); the epics are RUN.md and `docs/plan/`.

— *Rejected:*
- the three rules in this task — above;
- leaving `shutdownAction` out — a closed window would stop the container the executor runs in, and the next move, which compares `.devcontainer/` with the template byte for byte, would meet a difference no decision holds.

— *Consequence:* the author rebuilds the container after the merge, and the orchestrator waits for it before the next task. The merge releases the next patch with the same code and schema, though only comments of the Dockerfile change: the release's `paths` do not tell a comment from a line. The Dockerfile's hash changes, so the first push to `main` builds the image and saves new entries, and until then a pull request misses them. — `spec/`; `.devcontainer/`; CLAUDE.md, README.md, RUN.md; [README.md](README.md); [architecture.md](architecture.md); `docs/decisions.md`; [run/QUESTIONS.md](../run/QUESTIONS.md); [run/DEBT.md](../run/DEBT.md); [plan/E8.md](plan/E8.md).

## The three rules in place of a calibration

**R45. The model holds the three rules in place of a calibration. A module's level starts a band's width below the band of the learner's grade, below the lowest band too. An adult's correction — evidence of kind 3, which `LevelCorrection` gives — sets the level at the middle of the band the adult chose, in one step, and keeps it when the level stands in that band already; either way it ends the trial series and starts the guard's count anew. Three failures of the module in a row past its trial series lower the level by a band: a failure is a piece of evidence on which the model expected the learner to know at least half of the answers, above the guessing floor, and fewer than half of the items with an answer were solved at the end of the attempt. `Read` gives the band the level stands in. The state is of kind 2. `sim` judged the three rules at the commit of their pull request, and the gate stands on `sim`'s tag `v0.1.28`, with the bands recorded on that commit.** A task outside an epic, written by the text the orchestrator gave for a row of `spec`'s plan, with `spec`'s addition of the band `Read` gives, in the run of the whole «Порядок» by the orchestrator. Where each rule lives and its numbers — a band below, a band in one step, three failures — are `spec`'s, set with the rules; what a failure is, `spec` leaves to this log, and the rest is the execution's.
- **The start.** `BandByGrade` gives the band of the grade, as before, and the evidence carries it as the start band and the state pins it: the band the start is counted from, as `spec` counts the start from the grade's band. θ₀ = 2.5 · (p − 1) − 2.5, so a learner of the lowest band's grades, or with no grade, starts at −2.5, the place below the lowest band that the scale of bands had not. The trial series centres its prior there, and `Readout.Task` reads a module with no evidence from there.
- **The band the level stands in**, `ModuleReadout.LevelBand` with `HasLevelBand`: the band of the module's current version whose middle task, at 2.5 · (p − 1), is nearest the level, within half a band, the upper of two as near; the highest band when the level is above it; none when the level is more than 1.25 below the lowest band's middle, where the lowest band's learners start, and none without a current version or bands. For a module with no evidence it is the band of the starting point. It is the `level_band` of the skill map and what `api`'s check of `SetModuleLevel` holds the band of a correction next to.
- **The correction**, `LevelCorrection(module, band, start, at)`: kind 3 adds `level_set_by_adult`, field 21. Its record names no task — `task_id` `<module>/level/<band ID>`, the band set as `task_band`, difficulty 3, the adult as both provenances — and holds no skills, items, traps or other facts of an attempt, and the codec and `Append` refuse a correction other than it gives. `Append`: θ becomes the band's offset unless it stands in the band set already; the trial series ends if it lasts (`TrialEndedByAdult`, its answers cleared), the module's last evidence is not in it, and the count of the guard is 0, so that a repeat changes nothing. No skill moves, n stays, and its time is no practice. A correction as a module's first evidence creates the module, with the evidence's start band. A band above the current version's highest sets the level at the highest, so that a correction logged on another version still folds, as every log must on any version, and a band is read by its place, as an attempt's is; a module with no current version waits, as with any evidence. The library does not check that the band is next to the current one: `api`'s method does, and a log that `wrong_key` cut may put a correction after another level than the one it was made at, which the recomputation must still read.
- **The guard.** Past the trial series, a piece of evidence that moves the module is a failure when the sum of R̄ · σ of its answers — their P above the guessing floor, from the state before it, as the step takes them — is at least half their number, and fewer than half of the items with an answer were solved at the end: the final verdict right, or the first with no final one recorded; an item "not solved" by a shown solution is not. `FailuresInARow` of the module counts them; the third lowers θ by 2.5 after the step, but not below −2.5 nor upward from below it; the count goes on, lowering no more, until a piece of evidence that is no failure starts it at 0. The trial series counts nothing, and a correction starts the count anew.
- **The state** is of kind 2: `failures_in_a_row` and `trial_ended_by_adult`, fields 9 and 10 of a module, written from kind 2, required there and refused in kind 1. `api` recomputes its cache on a new version of the library anyway, since the cache is marked with it. Evidence is of kind 3.
- **The judgement of `sim`.** `sim`'s judgement of the three rules, a row of `spec`'s plan, ran on the commit of the pull request, df338e5; then `sim` cut its tag `v0.1.28` with the bands of that commit, and `SIM_TAG` and `SIM_COMMIT` of the gate name it. On 2,000 learners a cell `sim`'s short run held two of its four conditions — `M11.same` of grade 5, 0.812 against the base's 0.5525, and the guard's false firings of grade 3, 0.0005 against at most 0.05 — and not `fair`, one of its six checks, nor the correction against `D-grade1`; the correction held against the truth. The failure of `fair` is part of the residual risk that the DPIA's sign-off accepts. A rough look on 300 learners a cell named no failure of `fair`, and many "no worse" checks failing by the start (measurements 9.3): it did not see it.

— *Why:*
- **The start band stays the grade's.** Every log then starts its modules by the new rule on recomputation, the evidence keeps one meaning for the start band, and no place 0 enters the evidence, whose places start at 1. `spec` counts the start from the grade's band.
- **The failure is the model's own expectation and the end of the attempt**, not a skill's failure, fewer than half right at the first verdict. In the corridor a learner at its level fails about 22.5 % of first answers (the target 0.775), and three in a row come to 36 % of such learners within 50 answers and to 84 % within 200 — a computation of runs, measurements 9.1 —, against the at most 5 % of false firings by which `sim`'s short run judges the guard. On `sim`'s gate, "stuck at the end" alone fired 12,886 times, and `M1.rms.200` of `G0` in olympiad maths went from 0.50 to 1.50: most of the attempts stuck on were tasks the model itself already gave a P near 0, far below the corridor of `engine` (measurements 9.2). Such a task says nothing of the level. A task the model gave as fitting that the learner could not solve even on the ladder says that the level stands too high, which is what the guard is for.
- **Above the guessing floor.** P is c + (1 − c) · R̄ · σ, never below c, so with P a choice of two, c = ½, would be fitting however far above the level; R̄ · σ is the chance the learner knows the answer, and with no guessing it is P.
- **One lowering a run.** Without it a learner stuck on a run of tasks that stay hard for it sinks a band each three to the floor.
- **The middle of the band, the nearest band.** The middle task of a band, difficulty 3, stands at its offset, so the correction puts the level where the band's tasks are in the corridor, and the band it stands in is then the one set; the nearest middle makes "Easier" from a level near the next band's middle a step of about a band, not of almost two.
- **The correction ends the series.** The series finds the start; the adult gave it. Left on, the series' next answer would compute θ anew from θ₀ and undo the correction.
- **The form of the record.** An earlier version reads a new form, skipping what it does not know: one before kind 3 skips field 21 and refuses evidence whose task names no module, so the record carries a task ID that names the module and nothing an earlier version reads as an answer, and it moves nothing there.

— *Rejected:*
- a failure by a skill's rule, the first verdict, one "stuck at the end" without the model's expectation, and the expectation by P with its guessing floor — above;
- a correction to a band the current version does not have refused — a log written on a version with more bands could no longer be recomputed;
- a start band a band lower in the evidence, `BandByGrade` giving the band below or a place 0 below the lowest — the start band would mean two things, a place 0 changes the checks of the evidence, and the logs before kind 3 would keep the former start on recomputation;
- a correction that keeps the series, its centre moved to the band set — a new field of the state, and five more answers would move the level a band either way, against "in one step";
- a record with a field of its own for the module and no task — an earlier version refuses it, and an earlier version must read a new form;
- the adjacency of the band in the library — above;
- a guard with no floor — a learner stuck on everything would sink without bound.

— *Consequence:* what the model computes changes, so "Gate" stands on the bands `sim` recorded for this change. — `rules.go`, `model.go`, `evidence.go`, `state.go`, `readout.go`, the codec; `proto/`; `testdata/` — the golden files written anew, a new golden log `rules` and the golden correction `testdata/wire/correction.binpb`; `.github/workflows/ci.yml`; [architecture.md](architecture.md); [measurements.md](measurements.md); CLAUDE.md, README.md; `docs/decisions.md`; [run/DEBT.md](../run/DEBT.md); [run/QUESTIONS.md](../run/QUESTIONS.md); [plan/E8.md](plan/E8.md).

## The context of E8's sessions

**R47. E8's sessions carried more context a call than E7's because they were longer, not because of the documents: over each session's first 80 main calls E8 carries 122.1 thousand a call, 4.5 % below the 127.9 of E7's five sessions, and its three sessions of 111, 138 and 234 main calls are the whole excess over the reference. What entered from the documents is E7's in size. So `docs-budgets.json`, the order of reading that RUN.md sets and the documents stay as they are; what `spec`'s threshold for an analysis and the waits for long runs needed went to `spec` as two rows of table 3, and `spec` answered both by their recommendations: a run longer than a few minutes goes to the background, as the shared rules say, and `tokens-report` prints the context of each session's first 80 main calls, with which an analysis by the threshold begins.** A task outside an epic, set by `spec`'s threshold for an analysis of an epic's sessions, which E8's row of the measurements met; its decisions are the executor's, in a run of `spec`'s orchestrator.
- **What was measured** (measurements 10, 2026-11-17): `tokens-report` over the windows of E7 and E8 and a script over the same transcripts for each main call by its number in the session. Every session starts at 26–40 thousand and grows over its first 60 calls by 1.7–3.0 thousand a call, E8's no faster than E7's; E7's longest has 79 main calls. What entered between calls by Bash is 1.28 million characters in E8 against 1.26 in E7, `docs/decisions.md` 294,693 against 293,746 by Bash, and RUN.md less; it carried more only because it rode on more calls after it. The task that laid out the epic also measured E7, the analysis and the exploration ran `sim`'s cells, and the exploration waited for 19 background runs in 76 main calls, $3.08 of its $14.62.
- **What was decided:**
  1. `docs-budgets.json` is neither raised nor lowered: the documents brought into E8's sessions what they brought into E7's, and a lower limit would cut what a task has to read, not the length that cost;
  2. RUN.md's rule on what a session reads first keeps its order: its reads come at the start of a session, where E8 is below E7;
  3. the length of a task stays for RUN.md's rule on a task larger than one review to judge. The analysis and the exploration were long by their runs — the exploration by its 48 sets screened and decided —, a split of an exploration into sessions brings each the documents again, and nothing here measures that it would be cheaper;
  4. how to wait is the shared rules' to say, not one repository's: it went to `spec`, and the shared rules now say it, so nothing here does;
  5. the threshold's mean tells the length of the sessions from the documents only by a figure that `tokens-report` did not print: it went to `spec`, and `tokens-report` prints it now.
- **Rejected:**
  - lower limits for `docs/decisions.md`'s entries or the sections of [architecture.md](architecture.md), or a split of the log: neither brought E8 more than E7, and an entry is never rewritten;
  - a sentence on waiting in RUN.md or in a rule of `.claude/rules/` here: no part's `paths` matches the temporary clone where an exploration runs `sim`, RUN.md keeps the template's rules but for this repository's layout of the plan, and a sentence of the shared rules reaches every repository whose task runs long;
  - E8 as a new reference: the reference is this repository's first row of at least 300 main calls after the translation of `docs/` into English, E7's 128.2 (measurements 1.9 spec), and a threshold met by length is no reason to move it.

— *Consequence:* nothing changes in the code, the tests, CI, the container or the budgets. — [measurements.md](measurements.md); `docs/decisions.md`; [run/QUESTIONS.md](../run/QUESTIONS.md); [plan/E8.md](plan/E8.md).
